## `main.py`

```python
"""
PROFIN — Personal & Project Finance Tracker
Entry point. Initialises DB, runs auth flow, launches main window.
"""

import logging
import sys
import traceback
from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtCore import QCoreApplication, Qt
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QMessageBox

from app.db.database import get_user_app_dir, init_db
from app.gui.login_window import LoginWindow


def _setup_logging() -> None:
    """
    Configure application-wide logging:
      - Console handler : INFO level,  compact format  → VSCode terminal
      - File handler    : DEBUG level, full format     → logs/profin.log
      - Rotating        : 1 MB max per file, 3 backups kept
      - Third-party     : sqlalchemy / PIL silenced at WARNING
    """
    from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler

    app_root = get_user_app_dir()
    log_dir = app_root / "logs"
    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    log_file = log_dir / "profin.log"

    root = logging.getLogger()
    root.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

    root.handlers.clear()

    console = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    console.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    console.setFormatter(logging.Formatter(
        fmt     = "%(asctime)s  %(levelname)-8s  %(name)s — %(message)s",
        datefmt = "%H:%M:%S",
    ))

    file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
        str(log_file),
        maxBytes    = 1_048_576,
        backupCount = 3,
        encoding    = "utf-8",
    )
    file_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
    file_handler.setFormatter(logging.Formatter(
        fmt     = "%(asctime)s  %(levelname)-8s  %(name)-40s  %(message)s",
        datefmt = "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
    ))

    root.addHandler(console)
    root.addHandler(file_handler)

    logging.getLogger("sqlalchemy.engine").setLevel(logging.WARNING)
    logging.getLogger("sqlalchemy.pool").setLevel(logging.WARNING)
    logging.getLogger("PIL").setLevel(logging.WARNING)

    logging.getLogger(__name__).info(
        "Logging iniciado — consola: INFO | fichero DEBUG: %s", log_file
    )


def _show_fatal_error(message: str, detailed: str | None = None) -> None:
    """Mostrar un QMessageBox con el error y registrar la traza."""
    logging.getLogger(__name__).exception(message)
    # If QApplication isn't created yet, try to create a temporary one to show the dialog.
    try:
        if QApplication.instance() is None:
            _tmp = QApplication([])
            QMessageBox.critical(None, "Error crítico - PROFIN", message + ("\n\n" + detailed if detailed else ""))
            _tmp.quit()
        else:
            QMessageBox.critical(None, "Error crítico - PROFIN", message + ("\n\n" + detailed if detailed else ""))
    except Exception:
        # Fallback to console output if QMessageBox fails for any reason
        print(message)
        if detailed:
            print(detailed)


def _unhandled_exception(exc_type, exc, tb) -> None:
    """Hook global para excepciones no capturadas: registra y muestra al usuario."""
    formatted = "".join(traceback.format_exception(exc_type, exc, tb))
    logging.getLogger(__name__).error("Unhandled exception:\n%s", formatted)
    # Mostrar un cuadro crítico con información mínima
    try:
        QMessageBox.critical(None, "Error inesperado", "Se ha producido un error inesperado. Por favor revisa los logs (logs/profin.log) y reinicia la aplicación.")
    except Exception:
        # Si no hay GUI disponible, imprimir al stdout
        print("Unhandled exception:")
        print(formatted)


def main() -> None:
    _setup_logging()
    # Registrar hook global de excepciones
    sys.excepthook = _unhandled_exception

    logging.getLogger(__name__).info(
        "PROFIN arrancando — Python %s", sys.version.split()[0]
    )

    # ── HiDPI: mejor visual en pantallas de alta densidad ─────────────
    QCoreApplication.setAttribute(Qt.AA_EnableHighDpiScaling, True)
    QCoreApplication.setAttribute(Qt.AA_UseHighDpiPixmaps, True)

    # ── Inicializar DB de forma segura y notificar si falla ──────────
    try:
        init_db()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - queremos capturar cualquier fallo aquí para informar al usuario
        tb = traceback.format_exc()
        _show_fatal_error("No se pudo inicializar la base de datos.", tb)
        return

    # ── Crear la aplicación Qt y lanzar la ventana de login ───────────
    qt_app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    qt_app.setApplicationName("PROFIN")
    # Fusion es fiable; el tema y paleta pueden personalizarse en app/gui/theme.py
    qt_app.setStyle("Fusion")

    window = LoginWindow()
    window.show()

    # Guardar la excepción global también en el bucle de eventos (por si Qt intercepta algo)
    return_code = qt_app.exec()
    logging.getLogger(__name__).info("PROFIN terminado con código %s", return_code)
    sys.exit(return_code)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## `app/db/models.py`

```python
"""
SQLAlchemy ORM models for PROFIN.

Hierarchy:
  users
    └── accounts          (one per user in v1, multi-account ready)
    └── categories        (self-referencing tree: type → category → subcategory)
    └── import_batches
          └── transactions
    └── auto_categorization_rules
    └── audit_log
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import enum
from datetime import datetime, date
from typing import Optional, List

from sqlalchemy import (
    Boolean,
    Date,
    DateTime,
    Enum,
    Float,
    ForeignKey,
    Integer,
    String,
    Text,
    UniqueConstraint,
    func,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Mapped, mapped_column, relationship


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Base
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Enums
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class TransactionType(str, enum.Enum):
    income = "income"
    expense = "expense"
    investment = "investment"
    unknown = "unknown"


class CategoryLevel(str, enum.Enum):
    type = "type"
    category = "category"
    subcategory = "subcategory"


class MatchType(str, enum.Enum):
    contains = "contains"
    regex = "regex"
    exact = "exact"


class AuditAction(str, enum.Enum):
    insert = "insert"
    update = "update"
    delete = "delete"


class ImportStatus(str, enum.Enum):
    pending = "pending"
    completed = "completed"
    failed = "failed"
    partial = "partial"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# users
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    username: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=False)
    # bcrypt embeds the salt in the hash — stored for reference/auditing
    salt: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.now(), nullable=False
    )
    last_login: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    # Relationships
    accounts: Mapped[List[Account]] = relationship(
        "Account", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )
    categories: Mapped[List[Category]] = relationship(
        "Category", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )
    rules: Mapped[List[AutoCategorizationRule]] = relationship(
        "AutoCategorizationRule", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )
    import_batches: Mapped[List[ImportBatch]] = relationship(
        "ImportBatch", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )
    audit_logs: Mapped[List[AuditLog]] = relationship(
        "AuditLog", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return f"<User id={self.id} username={self.username!r}>"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# accounts
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Account(Base):
    __tablename__ = "accounts"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    user_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    account_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    iban_last4: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(4), nullable=True)
    bank: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=False, default="CaixaBank"
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.now(), nullable=False
    )

    # Relationships
    user: Mapped[User] = relationship("User", back_populates="accounts")
    transactions: Mapped[List[Transaction]] = relationship(
        "Transaction", back_populates="account", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return f"<Account id={self.id} name={self.account_name!r} user_id={self.user_id}>"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# categories  (self-referencing tree)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Category(Base):
    __tablename__ = "categories"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
    )
    parent_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("categories.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)

    level: Mapped[CategoryLevel] = mapped_column(
        Enum(CategoryLevel), nullable=False
    )
    transaction_type: Mapped[Optional[TransactionType]] = mapped_column(
        Enum(TransactionType), nullable=True
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.now(), nullable=False
    )

    # Relationships
    user: Mapped[User] = relationship("User", back_populates="categories")
    parent: Mapped[Optional[Category]] = relationship(
        "Category", remote_side="Category.id", back_populates="children"
    )
    children: Mapped[List[Category]] = relationship(
        "Category", back_populates="parent", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )
    transactions: Mapped[List[Transaction]] = relationship(
        "Transaction", back_populates="category",
        passive_deletes=True,
    )
    rules: Mapped[List[AutoCategorizationRule]] = relationship(
        "AutoCategorizationRule", back_populates="category",
        passive_deletes=True,
    )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return f"<Category id={self.id} name={self.name!r} level={self.level}>"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# import_batches
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class ImportBatch(Base):
    __tablename__ = "import_batches"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
    )
    filename: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=False)
    import_timestamp: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.now(), nullable=False
    )
    rows_imported: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    rows_skipped: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    status: Mapped[ImportStatus] = mapped_column(
        Enum(ImportStatus), nullable=False, default=ImportStatus.pending
    )

    # Relationships
    user: Mapped[User] = relationship("User", back_populates="import_batches")
    transactions: Mapped[List[Transaction]] = relationship(
        "Transaction", back_populates="import_batch"
    )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"<ImportBatch id={self.id} file={self.filename!r} "
            f"status={self.status} rows={self.rows_imported}>"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# transactions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Transaction(Base):
    """
    Central fact table. One row = one bank movement.

    Raw CaixaBank fields (all persisted for traceability):
      transaction_date  ← Fecha (Col 0)
      value_date        ← Fecha valor (Col 1)
      amount            ← unified: positive=income, negative=expense (Col 6 or 7)
      running_balance   ← Saldo (Col 8 or 9 — confirm on first real import)
      concepto_comun    ← Concepto común code (Col 10-ish)
      concepto_propio   ← Concepto propio code
      issuer_name       ← Extracted from Col 14 (date + issuer pattern)
      concept           ← Concatenation of Cols 15–21

    Derived fields:
      type              ← Derived from amount sign + rule overrides
      category_id       ← Auto-assigned by rules or manual
    """

    __tablename__ = "transactions"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
    )
    account_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("accounts.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
    )
    import_batch_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("import_batches.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    category_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("categories.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )

    # --- Core date & amount fields ---
    transaction_date: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)
    value_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=True)
    amount: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=False)
    running_balance: Mapped[Optional[float]] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)

    # --- Derived / enriched fields ---
    type: Mapped[TransactionType] = mapped_column(
        Enum(TransactionType), nullable=False, default=TransactionType.unknown
    )
    issuer_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=True)
    concept: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    # --- Raw CaixaBank metadata fields (all stored for future use) ---
    concepto_comun: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    concepto_propio: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    # Raw concatenation of cols 15–21 before cleaning
    raw_details: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    # Col 14 raw value (date + issuer string as received from bank)
    raw_col14: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    # --- Import tracking ---
    source_file: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=True)
    is_duplicate_flagged: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False
    )
    duplicate_resolved_by_user: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False
    )

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.now(), nullable=False
    )

    # Relationships
    account: Mapped[Account] = relationship("Account", back_populates="transactions")
    import_batch: Mapped[Optional[ImportBatch]] = relationship(
        "ImportBatch", back_populates="transactions"
    )
    category: Mapped[Optional[Category]] = relationship(
        "Category", back_populates="transactions"
    )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"<Transaction id={self.id} date={self.transaction_date} "
            f"amount={self.amount} type={self.type}>"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# auto_categorization_rules
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class AutoCategorizationRule(Base):
    __tablename__ = "auto_categorization_rules"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    user_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    category_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("categories.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
    )
    match_pattern: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False)
    match_type: Mapped[MatchType] = mapped_column(
        Enum(MatchType), nullable=False, default=MatchType.contains
    )
    # Lower number = higher priority
    priority: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=100)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.now(), nullable=False
    )

    # Relationships
    user: Mapped[Optional[User]] = relationship("User", back_populates="rules")
    category: Mapped[Category] = relationship("Category", back_populates="rules")

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"<AutoCategorizationRule id={self.id} pattern={self.match_pattern!r} "
            f"type={self.match_type}>"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# audit_log
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class AuditLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "audit_log"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    user_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    table_affected: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    record_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    action: Mapped[AuditAction] = mapped_column(Enum(AuditAction), nullable=False)
    old_value: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    new_value: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    timestamp: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, server_default=func.now(), nullable=False
    )

    # Relationships
    user: Mapped[Optional[User]] = relationship("User", back_populates="audit_logs")

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"<AuditLog id={self.id} table={self.table_affected!r} "
            f"action={self.action} record_id={self.record_id}>"
        )
```

## `app/db/database.py`

```python
"""Database initialisation, integrity checks, recovery, and session factory."""

import gc
import logging
import os
import shutil
import sqlite3
import sys
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtCore import QSettings
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.db.models import Base, User

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def get_legacy_data_dir() -> Path:
    """Resolve the repo-local data folder used by development builds.

    In packaged builds the module is nested under a dist/_internal tree, so a
    simple parents[2] lookup points to a bundle-local folder instead of the real
    repository checkout. Search the local ancestry for a data/ directory that
    actually contains the PROFIN database so legacy user data can still be found.
    """
    module_path = Path(__file__).resolve()
    candidates: list[Path] = []

    for base in [module_path, module_path.parent, *module_path.parents]:
        candidates.extend(
            [
                base,
                base / "data",
                base / "PROFIN" / "data",
            ]
        )
    for base in [Path.cwd(), Path.cwd().parent, *Path.cwd().parents]:
        candidates.extend(
            [
                base,
                base / "data",
                base / "PROFIN" / "data",
            ]
        )
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        exe_dir = Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent if sys.executable else None
        if exe_dir is not None:
            for base in [exe_dir, *exe_dir.parents]:
                candidates.extend([base, base / "data", base / "PROFIN" / "data"])

    seen: set[Path] = set()
    for candidate in candidates:
        resolved = candidate.resolve(strict=False)
        if resolved in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(resolved)
        db_file = resolved / "profin.db" if resolved.is_dir() else None
        if db_file and db_file.exists():
            return resolved

    fallback = (module_path.parents[2] / "data").resolve()
    return fallback


def get_default_data_dir() -> Path:
    """Default writable location for the user profile on Windows."""
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        base_dir = os.environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA")
        root = Path(base_dir) if base_dir else Path.home() / "AppData" / "Local"
        return (root / "PROFIN" / "data").resolve()
    return get_legacy_data_dir()


def get_user_app_dir() -> Path:
    """Return the writable app directory for the current execution mode."""
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        local_app_data = os.environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA")
        base_dir = Path(local_app_data) if local_app_data else Path.home() / "AppData" / "Local"
        return (base_dir / "PROFIN").resolve()
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2].resolve()


def _database_has_users(db_path: Path) -> bool:
    """Return True when the SQLite database contains at least one user row."""
    if not db_path.exists():
        return False
    connection = None
    try:
        connection = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        table_exists = connection.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='users'"
        ).fetchone()
        if not table_exists:
            return False
        count = connection.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users").fetchone()[0]
        return int(count or 0) > 0
    except sqlite3.DatabaseError:
        return False
    finally:
        if connection is not None:
            connection.close()


def _migrate_legacy_database_if_needed(default_data_dir: Path, legacy_data_dir: Path) -> bool:
    """Copy a populated legacy DB into the packaged app folder if the current target is empty or stale."""
    legacy_db = legacy_data_dir / "profin.db"
    target_db = default_data_dir / "profin.db"
    if not legacy_db.exists():
        return False
    if _database_has_users(target_db):
        return False

    if "engine" in globals():
        engine.dispose()
    if "SessionLocal" in globals():
        SessionLocal.close_all()
    gc.collect()

    default_data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for suffix in ("", "-wal", "-shm"):
        source = legacy_data_dir / f"profin.db{suffix}"
        target = default_data_dir / f"profin.db{suffix}"
        if source.exists():
            if target.exists():
                target.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            shutil.copy2(source, target)
    return True


def get_user_data_dir() -> Path:
    """Return the writable data directory, preferring the user-configured value when set."""
    settings = QSettings("GEVernova", "PROFIN")
    default_data_dir = get_default_data_dir()
    legacy_data_dir = get_legacy_data_dir()

    configured = settings.value("settings/data_folder", "", type=str)
    if configured:
        data_dir = Path(configured).expanduser().resolve()
        if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
            if data_dir.resolve() == legacy_data_dir.resolve() or not _database_has_users(data_dir / "profin.db"):
                if _database_has_users(default_data_dir / "profin.db") or _database_has_users(legacy_data_dir / "profin.db"):
                    _migrate_legacy_database_if_needed(default_data_dir, legacy_data_dir)
                    settings.setValue("settings/data_folder", str(default_data_dir))
                    settings.sync()
                    return default_data_dir
        data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return data_dir

    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False) and legacy_data_dir.exists() and legacy_data_dir != default_data_dir:
        if _migrate_legacy_database_if_needed(default_data_dir, legacy_data_dir):
            settings.setValue("settings/data_folder", str(default_data_dir))
            settings.sync()
    default_data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return default_data_dir


def refresh_runtime_paths() -> Path:
    """Refresh the live DB location after a user moves or configures the data directory."""
    global DB_DIR, DB_PATH, DATABASE_URL, engine
    DB_DIR = get_user_data_dir()
    DB_PATH = DB_DIR / "profin.db"
    DATABASE_URL = f"sqlite:///{DB_PATH}"
    if "engine" in globals():
        engine.dispose()
    engine = _build_engine(DATABASE_URL)
    SessionLocal.configure(bind=engine)
    return DB_DIR


DB_DIR = get_user_data_dir()
DB_PATH = DB_DIR / "profin.db"
DATABASE_URL = f"sqlite:///{DB_PATH}"


def _set_sqlite_pragma(dbapi_conn, _connection_record) -> None:
    """Enable foreign keys and WAL mode for every new connection."""
    cursor = dbapi_conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
    cursor.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
    cursor.close()


def _build_engine(database_url: str = DATABASE_URL):
    database_engine = create_engine(
        database_url,
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        echo=False,
    )
    event.listen(database_engine, "connect", _set_sqlite_pragma)
    return database_engine


engine = _build_engine()
SessionLocal: sessionmaker[Session] = sessionmaker(
    bind=engine, autocommit=False, autoflush=False
)


def get_session() -> Session:
    """Return a new SQLAlchemy session. Caller is responsible for closing it."""
    return SessionLocal()


# New installations start with no accounts. Users are created explicitly from
# the registration flow instead of receiving shared demo credentials.
DEFAULT_BOOTSTRAP_USERS: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()


def _sqlite_integrity_errors(path: Path) -> list[str]:
    """Return SQLite integrity errors, or a diagnostic if the file is unreadable."""
    try:
        with sqlite3.connect(path) as connection:
            return [
                row[0]
                for row in connection.execute("PRAGMA integrity_check")
                if row[0] != "ok"
            ]
    except sqlite3.DatabaseError as exc:
        return [str(exc)]


def _copy_recoverable_rows(
    source: sqlite3.Connection, target: sqlite3.Connection
) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Copy readable rows into a clean database, skipping invalid rows only."""
    table_order = (
        "users",
        "accounts",
        "categories",
        "import_batches",
        "transactions",
        "auto_categorization_rules",
        "audit_log",
    )
    copied: dict[str, int] = {}
    target.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF")

    for table in table_order:
        try:
            source_columns = [
                row[1] for row in source.execute(f'PRAGMA table_info("{table}")')
            ]
            target_columns = [
                row[1] for row in target.execute(f'PRAGMA table_info("{table}")')
            ]
            columns = [column for column in source_columns if column in target_columns]
            if not columns:
                continue

            quoted = ", ".join(f'"{column}"' for column in columns)
            rows = source.execute(f'SELECT {quoted} FROM "{table}" ORDER BY rowid')
            count = 0
            for row in rows:
                try:
                    target.execute(
                        f'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO "{table}" ({quoted}) '
                        f"VALUES ({', '.join('?' for _ in columns)})",
                        row,
                    )
                    count += target.execute("SELECT changes()").fetchone()[0]
                except sqlite3.DatabaseError:
                    logger.warning("Fila no recuperable omitida: tabla=%s", table)
            copied[table] = count
        except sqlite3.DatabaseError as exc:
            logger.error("No se pudo recuperar la tabla %s: %s", table, exc)

    target.commit()
    target.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
    return copied


def _recover_corrupt_database(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Replace a corrupt SQLite file with a validated recoverable copy."""
    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    backup_path = DB_DIR / f"profin.corrupt.{timestamp}.db"
    recovery_path = DB_DIR / f"profin.recovery.{timestamp}.db"
    shutil.copy2(DB_PATH, backup_path)

    recovery_engine = _build_engine(f"sqlite:///{recovery_path}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=recovery_engine)
    recovery_engine.dispose()

    with sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) as source, sqlite3.connect(recovery_path) as target:
        copied = _copy_recoverable_rows(source, target)
        target.execute("VACUUM")

    recovery_errors = _sqlite_integrity_errors(recovery_path)
    if recovery_errors:
        recovery_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise RuntimeError(
            "La reparación no superó integrity_check: "
            + "; ".join(recovery_errors[:3])
        )

    engine.dispose()
    DB_PATH.unlink()
    recovery_path.replace(DB_PATH)
    logger.warning(
        "Base SQLite reparada. Copia original=%s filas=%s diagnóstico=%s",
        backup_path,
        copied,
        errors[:3],
    )


def _ensure_database_integrity() -> None:
    errors = _sqlite_integrity_errors(DB_PATH)
    if errors:
        _recover_corrupt_database(errors)


def _refresh_database_bindings() -> None:
    global engine
    engine.dispose()
    engine = _build_engine()
    SessionLocal.configure(bind=engine)


def ensure_demo_users(session: Session) -> list[User]:
    """Keep the legacy bootstrap hook without creating automatic demo users."""
    if session.query(User).count() > 0:
        return []

    from app.auth.auth_service import create_user

    created: list[User] = []
    for username, password in DEFAULT_BOOTSTRAP_USERS:
        try:
            created.append(create_user(session, username, password))
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return created


def init_db() -> None:
    """Create tables, repair corruption, and seed first-run credentials."""
    refresh_runtime_paths()
    if DB_PATH.exists():
        _ensure_database_integrity()
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    _refresh_database_bindings()
    with SessionLocal() as session:
        ensure_demo_users(session)
```

## `app/auth/auth_service.py`

```python
"""
app/auth/auth_service.py
=========================
PROFIN — Authentication Service

Provides three public functions:
  create_user(session, username, password) -> User
  verify_login(session, username, password) -> User | None
  get_user_by_id(session, user_id) -> User | None

Password hashing uses bcrypt directly (rounds=12).
passlib dependency removed in v0.0.3 due to bcrypt >= 4.x incompatibility.

Changelog:
  v0.0.3  Replaced passlib with direct bcrypt calls.
          Removed is_active field (not in User model v0.0.2).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Optional

import bcrypt
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.models import User

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Internal helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _hash_password(plain: str) -> str:
    """
    Hash a plaintext password using bcrypt (rounds=12).
    Returns a UTF-8 decoded hash string for storage in the DB.
    """
    return bcrypt.hashpw(
        plain.encode("utf-8"),
        bcrypt.gensalt(rounds=12),
    ).decode("utf-8")


def _verify_password(plain: str, hashed: str) -> bool:
    """
    Verify a plaintext password against a stored bcrypt hash.
    Returns True if the password matches, False otherwise.
    Safe against all bcrypt exceptions.
    """
    try:
        return bcrypt.checkpw(
            plain.encode("utf-8"),
            hashed.encode("utf-8"),
        )
    except Exception:
        return False


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def create_user(session: Session, username: str, password: str) -> User:
    """
    Create and persist a new User with a hashed password.

    Parameters
    ----------
    session  : Active SQLAlchemy session.
    username : Must be non-empty string. Uniqueness enforced at DB level.
    password : Must be non-empty string. Stored as bcrypt hash only.

    Returns
    -------
    User ORM object (already committed to the session).

    Raises
    ------
    ValueError : If username or password is empty.
    ValueError : If username already exists (duplicate).
    """
    if not username or not username.strip():
        raise ValueError("El nombre de usuario no puede estar vacío.")
    if not password:
        raise ValueError("La contraseña no puede estar vacía.")

    username      = username.strip()
    password_hash = _hash_password(password)

    user = User(
        username      = username,
        password_hash = password_hash,
        created_at    = datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    session.add(user)

    try:
        session.commit()
        session.refresh(user)
        logger.info("Usuario creado: '%s' (id=%s)", username, user.id)
        return user

    except IntegrityError:
        session.rollback()
        raise ValueError(
            f"El usuario '{username}' ya existe. "
            "Elige un nombre de usuario diferente."
        )


def verify_login(
    session:  Session,
    username: str,
    password: str,
) -> Optional[User]:
    """
    Verify login credentials and update last_login on success.

    Returns
    -------
    User object if credentials are valid, None otherwise.
    """
    if not username or not password:
        return None

    user: Optional[User] = (
        session.query(User)
        .filter(User.username == username.strip())
        .first()
    )

    if user is None:
        logger.debug("Login fallido: usuario '%s' no encontrado.", username)
        return None

    if not _verify_password(password, user.password_hash):
        logger.debug("Login fallido: contraseña incorrecta para '%s'.", username)
        return None

    # Update last_login
    user.last_login = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    try:
        session.commit()
    except Exception as exc:
        session.rollback()
        logger.warning(
            "No se pudo actualizar last_login para '%s': %s", username, exc
        )

    logger.info("Login exitoso: '%s' (id=%s)", username, user.id)
    return user


def get_user_by_id(session: Session, user_id: int) -> Optional[User]:
    """Retrieve a user by primary key. Returns None if not found."""
    return session.get(User, user_id)
```

## `app/auth/session_manager.py`

```python
"""
In-process session manager for PROFIN.

Holds the currently authenticated user for the lifetime of the application.
A new login clears any previous session. Session is cleared on app exit.

This is intentionally simple (single-user, single-process) — no tokens,
no persistence between restarts.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Optional
from app.db.models import User


class SessionManager:
    """Singleton-style in-memory session store."""

    _current_user: Optional[User] = None

    @classmethod
    def login(cls, user: User) -> None:
        """Set the active session user."""
        cls._current_user = user

    @classmethod
    def logout(cls) -> None:
        """Clear the active session."""
        cls._current_user = None

    @classmethod
    def get_current_user(cls) -> Optional[User]:
        """Return the currently logged-in user, or None."""
        return cls._current_user

    @classmethod
    def is_authenticated(cls) -> bool:
        """Return True if a user is currently logged in."""
        return cls._current_user is not None

    @classmethod
    def require_user(cls) -> User:
        """
        Return the current user or raise RuntimeError.
        Use in GUI handlers where a logged-in user is always expected.
        """
        if cls._current_user is None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "No hay ningún usuario autenticado. "
                "Por favor, inicia sesión antes de continuar."
            )
        return cls._current_user
```

## `app/ingestion/excel_parser.py`

```python
"""
app/ingestion/excel_parser.py
==============================
PROFIN — CaixaBank Excel Parser

Responsibility:
  Read a CaixaBank .xlsx export file (no traditional header row) and return
  a clean, typed pandas DataFrame with standardised column names ready for
  validation and import.

CaixaBank file structure (24 unnamed columns, data from row 1 onward):
  Col 0  : always NaN                     → dropped
  Col 1  : IBAN full string               → iban_raw
  Col 2  : branch code                    → dropped
  Col 3  : currency (EUR)                 → currency
  Col 4  : operation date  dd/mm/yyyy     → transaction_date
  Col 5  : value date      dd/mm/yyyy     → value_date
  Col 6  : ingreso amount  (NaN if gasto) → _ingreso_raw
  Col 7  : gasto amount    (NaN if ingr.) → _gasto_raw
  Col 8  : running balance                → running_balance
  Col 9  : always NaN                     → dropped
  Col 10 : concepto común code            → concepto_comun
  Col 11 : concepto propio code           → concepto_propio
  Col 12 : always 000000000000            → dropped
  Col 13 : bank reference                 → dropped
  Col 14 : issuer / operation description → issuer_name
  Col 15-22 : complementary details       → concept  (concatenated, stripped)
  Col 23 : NaN / extra                    → dropped

Output columns (standardised):
  transaction_date  : datetime.date
  value_date        : datetime.date | None
  amount            : float  (positive = income, negative = expense)
  running_balance   : float
  concepto_comun    : str | None
  concepto_propio   : str | None
  issuer_name       : str | None
  concept           : str | None  (concat of cols 14-22)
  raw_col14         : str | None  (col 14 exactly as received)
  currency          : str         (always 'EUR' for CaixaBank ES)
  iban_raw          : str | None
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Union

import pandas as pd


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Column indices (0-based) in the raw CaixaBank .xlsx
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
_COL_IBAN            = 1
_COL_CURRENCY        = 3
_COL_DATE_OP         = 4
_COL_DATE_VAL        = 5
_COL_INGRESO         = 6
_COL_GASTO           = 7
_COL_SALDO           = 8
_COL_CONCEPTO_COMUN  = 10
_COL_CONCEPTO_PROPIO = 11
_COL_DETAILS_START   = 14   # issuer + details cols 14-22
_COL_DETAILS_END     = 22   # inclusive

# Row offset handled dynamically by preamble detection in parse_excel()
# _DATA_START_ROW removed — CaixaBank .xls has variable preamble length

# Date format used by CaixaBank
_DATE_FORMAT = "%d/%m/%Y"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def parse_excel(file_path: Union[str, Path]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Read a CaixaBank .xlsx export and return a clean, typed DataFrame.

    Parameters
    ----------
    file_path : str or Path
        Path to the .xlsx file.

    Returns
    -------
    pd.DataFrame with columns:
        transaction_date, value_date, amount, running_balance,
        concepto_comun, concepto_propio, issuer_name, concept,
        raw_col14, currency, iban_raw

    Raises
    ------
    FileNotFoundError
        If the file does not exist.
    ValueError
        If the file cannot be read as a valid Excel workbook.
    """
    file_path = Path(file_path)

    if not file_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Fichero no encontrado: {file_path}"
        )

    # -- Read raw: no header, all columns as object to avoid type coercion --
    # Determine engine based on file extension
    suffix = Path(str(file_path)).suffix.lower().strip()
    if suffix == ".xls":
        _engine = "xlrd"
    elif suffix == ".xlsx":
        _engine = "openpyxl"
    else:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Formato de fichero no soportado: '{suffix}'. "
            "PROFIN acepta ficheros .xls y .xlsx de CaixaBank."
        )

    logger.info(
        "Leyendo fichero '%s' con engine='%s'",
        file_path.name, _engine
    )

    try:
        raw: pd.DataFrame = pd.read_excel(
            file_path,
            header=None,
            dtype=object,
            engine=_engine,        # ← solo una vez
        )
        
    except Exception as exc:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No se puede leer el fichero Excel '{file_path.name}': {exc}"
        ) from exc

    # -- Locate and strip preamble + real header row -----------------------
    # CaixaBank .xls structure (xlrd, header=None):
    #   row 0 → empty
    #   row 1 → "MOVIMIENTOS DESDE..." (title)
    #   row 2 → empty
    #   row 3 → "F. Operación | F. Valor | ..."  ← REAL HEADER
    #   row 4 → first transaction
    #   row N → empty (trailing)
    # We detect the header row by scanning for "F. Operaci" in the first
    # 10 rows (accent-insensitive, handles both .xls and .xlsx variants).

    header_row_idx: int | None = None
    for _i in range(min(10, len(raw))):
        _row_str = " ".join(str(v) for v in raw.iloc[_i].tolist()).lower()
        if "f. operaci" in _row_str or "f.operaci" in _row_str:
            header_row_idx = _i
            break

    if header_row_idx is not None:
        logger.info(
            "Preámbulo detectado — cabecera en fila %d "
            "(%d filas de preámbulo eliminadas)",
            header_row_idx, header_row_idx + 1,
        )
        raw = raw.iloc[header_row_idx + 1:].reset_index(drop=True)
    else:
        logger.warning(
            "Cabecera 'F. Operación' no encontrada en las primeras 10 filas "
            "— continuando sin eliminar preámbulo. "
            "Valor col[4] fila 0: %s",
            str(raw.iloc[0, 4]) if len(raw) > 0 else "N/A",
        )

    # -- Drop fully empty rows (trailing blank + any mid-file gaps) --------
    raw = raw.dropna(how="all").reset_index(drop=True)

    logger.info("Filas de datos tras limpieza: %d", len(raw))

    data = raw.copy()

    if data.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "El fichero no contiene filas de datos tras omitir la cabecera."
        )

    # -- Validate minimum column count -------------------------------------
    if data.shape[1] < 23:
        raise ValueError(
            f"El fichero tiene {data.shape[1]} columnas — "
            "se esperan al menos 23. "
            "¿Es un export de CaixaBank con el formato correcto?"
        )

    # -- Extract & clean each field ----------------------------------------
    out = pd.DataFrame()

    out["iban_raw"] = (
        data.iloc[:, _COL_IBAN]
        .astype(str)
        .str.strip()
        .replace("nan", None)
    )

    out["currency"] = (
        data.iloc[:, _COL_CURRENCY]
        .astype(str)
        .str.strip()
        .replace("nan", None)
    )

    out["transaction_date"] = _parse_dates(
        data.iloc[:, _COL_DATE_OP], "Fecha operación (col 4)"
    )
    out["value_date"] = _parse_dates(
        data.iloc[:, _COL_DATE_VAL], "Fecha valor (col 5)",
        required=False
    )

    # Unify ingreso / gasto → signed amount
    # ingreso (col 6): positive float, NaN if not an income row
    # gasto   (col 7): positive float, NaN if not an expense row
    # Rule: amount = +ingreso if ingreso not NaN, else -gasto
    ingreso = _to_numeric(data.iloc[:, _COL_INGRESO], "Ingreso (col 6)")
    gasto   = _to_numeric(data.iloc[:, _COL_GASTO],   "Gasto (col 7)")
    out["amount"] = ingreso.combine_first(-gasto)

    out["running_balance"] = _to_numeric(
        data.iloc[:, _COL_SALDO], "Saldo (col 8)"
    )

    out["concepto_comun"] = _clean_str(
        data.iloc[:, _COL_CONCEPTO_COMUN]
    )
    out["concepto_propio"] = _clean_str(
        data.iloc[:, _COL_CONCEPTO_PROPIO]
    )

    # raw_col14: col 14 exactly as received (before stripping)
    out["raw_col14"] = (
        data.iloc[:, 14]
        .astype(str)
        .str.strip()
        .replace("nan", None)
    )

    # issuer_name: extract from col 14 — remove leading "Fecha de operación: DD-MM-YYYY"
    out["issuer_name"] = out["raw_col14"].apply(_extract_issuer)

    # concept: concatenate cols 14-22 (strip, drop empty, join with space)
    concept_cols = data.iloc[
        :, _COL_DETAILS_START : _COL_DETAILS_END + 1
    ]
    out["concept"] = concept_cols.apply(
        _concat_details, axis=1
    )

    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Internal helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _parse_dates(
    series: pd.Series,
    field_name: str,
    required: bool = True,
) -> pd.Series:
    """
    Parse a date column from a CaixaBank export.

    Handles three input variants produced by different Excel engines:
      - datetime / Timestamp objects  → xlrd converts .xls dates automatically
      - string "dd/mm/yyyy"           → standard CaixaBank text format
      - string "yyyy-mm-dd ..."       → ISO format (edge cases / openpyxl)
      - NaN / None / "nan"            → treated as missing

    Returns a Series of datetime.date objects (or None if not required).
    """
    def _coerce_one(val: object) -> "pd.Timestamp | None":
        """Convert a single cell value to a Timestamp, or NaT on failure."""
        import datetime as _dt

        if val is None or (isinstance(val, float) and pd.isna(val)):
            return pd.NaT

        # Already a date/datetime object (xlrd auto-converts .xls date cells)
        if isinstance(val, (_dt.datetime, _dt.date)):
            return pd.Timestamp(val)

        # Pandas Timestamp
        if isinstance(val, pd.Timestamp):
            return val

        s = str(val).strip()
        if not s or s.lower() in ("nan", "none", "nat"):
            return pd.NaT

        # Try the canonical CaixaBank format first
        for fmt in ("%d/%m/%Y", "%d-%m-%Y", "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
                    "%Y-%m-%d", "%d/%m/%y", "%d-%m-%y"):
            try:
                return pd.Timestamp(
                    _dt.datetime.strptime(s[:len(fmt) + 2].strip(), fmt)
                )
            except ValueError:
                continue

        # Last resort — let pandas guess
        try:
            return pd.to_datetime(s, dayfirst=True)
        except Exception:
            return pd.NaT

    parsed = series.apply(_coerce_one)

    if required and parsed.isna().any():
        bad_count = int(parsed.isna().sum())
        # Log which rows are failing to help diagnose edge cases
        bad_vals = series[parsed.isna()].tolist()
        logger.warning(
            "_parse_dates '%s': %d unparseable value(s): %s",
            field_name, bad_count, bad_vals[:5]
        )
        raise ValueError(
            f"Campo '{field_name}': {bad_count} fila(s) con fecha "
            "no válida o formato inesperado (se esperaba dd/mm/yyyy). "
            f"Valores problemáticos: {[str(v) for v in bad_vals[:3]]}"
        )
    return parsed.dt.date


def _to_numeric(series: pd.Series, field_name: str) -> pd.Series:
    """
    Convert a column to float. Treats blank / 'nan' strings as NaN.
    Does NOT raise on NaN — callers decide if NaN is acceptable.
    """
    cleaned = (
        series.astype(str)
        .str.strip()
        .str.replace(",", ".", regex=False)  # handle European decimal comma
        .replace({"nan": None, "": None, "None": None})
    )
    return pd.to_numeric(cleaned, errors="coerce")


def _clean_str(series: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
    """Strip whitespace, collapse internal spaces, return None for blank/nan."""
    return (
        series.astype(str)
        .str.strip()
        .str.replace(r"\s+", " ", regex=True)
        .replace({"nan": None, "": None, "None": None})
    )


_DATE_PREFIX_RE = re.compile(
    r"^Fecha\s+de\s+operaci[oó]n\s*:\s*\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}\s*",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)


def _extract_issuer(raw: object) -> str | None:
    """
    Extract the issuer name from col 14.
    CaixaBank col 14 has two patterns:
      a) "Fecha de operación: DD-MM-YYYY   ISSUER NAME"
         → strip the date prefix, return stripped issuer name
      b) Plain issuer name with no date prefix
         → return as-is (stripped)
    Returns None if blank/nan.
    """
    if not raw or str(raw).strip() in ("", "nan", "None"):
        return None
    text = str(raw).strip()
    text = _DATE_PREFIX_RE.sub("", text).strip()
    # Collapse multiple internal spaces (CaixaBank pads with spaces)
    text = re.sub(r"\s{2,}", " ", text)
    return text if text else None


def _concat_details(row: pd.Series) -> str | None:
    """
    Concatenate detail columns 14-22 into a single concept string.
    Strips each cell, drops empty/nan, joins with single space.
    Returns None if all cells are empty.
    """
    parts = []
    for val in row:
        s = str(val).strip()
        if s and s not in ("nan", "None"):
            # Collapse internal spaces
            s = re.sub(r"\s{2,}", " ", s)
            parts.append(s)
    return " | ".join(parts) if parts else None
```

## `app/ingestion/validator.py`

```python
"""
app/ingestion/validator.py
===========================
PROFIN — Import Validation Layer

Validation rules (in order):
  V-01  DataFrame is not empty.
  V-02  All required output columns are present.
  V-03  transaction_date: no null values.
  V-04  amount: no null values.
  V-05  running_balance: no null values.
  V-06  currency: all rows are 'EUR'.
  V-07  iban_raw: all rows share the same IBAN.
  V-08  amount != 0: zero-amount rows flagged as warnings (not errors).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List

import pandas as pd


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Custom exceptions / result types
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class ValidationError(Exception):
    """Blocking validation failure. Message shown directly in GUI."""


@dataclass
class ValidationWarning:
    """Non-blocking warning surfaced to the user during import preview."""
    rule:        str
    message:     str
    row_indices: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class ValidationResult:
    """Returned by validate_dataframe() on success."""
    row_count: int
    iban:      str
    warnings:  List[ValidationWarning] = field(default_factory=list)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Required columns
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
_REQUIRED_COLUMNS = [
    "transaction_date",
    "value_date",
    "amount",
    "running_balance",
    "concepto_comun",
    "concepto_propio",
    "issuer_name",
    "concept",
    "raw_col14",
    "currency",
    "iban_raw",
]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def validate_dataframe(df: pd.DataFrame) -> ValidationResult:
    """
    Run all validation rules on a parsed DataFrame.

    Returns
    -------
    ValidationResult on success.

    Raises
    ------
    ValidationError on any blocking failure (V-01 to V-07).
    """
    warnings: List[ValidationWarning] = []

    # V-01 — Not empty
    if df.empty:
        raise ValidationError(
            "El fichero no contiene filas de datos. "
            "Comprueba que el export de CaixaBank no esté vacío."
        )

    # V-02 — Required columns present
    missing_cols = [c for c in _REQUIRED_COLUMNS if c not in df.columns]
    if missing_cols:
        raise ValidationError(
            "Columnas requeridas no encontradas tras el parseo: "
            + ", ".join(f"'{c}'" for c in missing_cols)
            + ". Puede indicar un cambio en el formato de export de CaixaBank."
        )

    # V-03 — transaction_date: no nulls
    null_dates = df["transaction_date"].isna()
    if null_dates.any():
        bad_rows = df.index[null_dates].tolist()
        raise ValidationError(
            f"'Fecha operación': {len(bad_rows)} fila(s) sin fecha válida "
            f"(filas: {bad_rows[:5]}{'...' if len(bad_rows) > 5 else ''}). "
            "Comprueba que todas las filas tengan fecha en formato dd/mm/yyyy."
        )

    # V-04 — amount: no nulls
    null_amounts = df["amount"].isna()
    if null_amounts.any():
        bad_rows = df.index[null_amounts].tolist()
        raise ValidationError(
            f"'Importe': {len(bad_rows)} fila(s) sin importe válido "
            f"(filas: {bad_rows[:5]}{'...' if len(bad_rows) > 5 else ''}). "
            "Cada fila debe tener un valor en Ingreso o Gasto."
        )

    # V-05 — running_balance: no nulls
    null_balance = df["running_balance"].isna()
    if null_balance.any():
        bad_rows = df.index[null_balance].tolist()
        raise ValidationError(
            f"'Saldo': {len(bad_rows)} fila(s) sin saldo válido "
            f"(filas: {bad_rows[:5]}{'...' if len(bad_rows) > 5 else ''}). "
            "Todas las filas deben tener un saldo numérico."
        )

    # V-06 — currency: EUR only
    non_eur = df[df["currency"] != "EUR"]
    if not non_eur.empty:
        bad_currencies = non_eur["currency"].unique().tolist()
        raise ValidationError(
            "PROFIN v1 soporta únicamente la divisa EUR. "
            f"Se encontraron: {bad_currencies} en {len(non_eur)} fila(s)."
        )

    # V-07 — iban_raw: single IBAN per file
    unique_ibans = df["iban_raw"].dropna().unique()
    if len(unique_ibans) == 0:
        raise ValidationError(
            "No se encontró ningún IBAN en el fichero. "
            "Comprueba que el export sea de CaixaBank."
        )
    if len(unique_ibans) > 1:
        raise ValidationError(
            f"El fichero contiene {len(unique_ibans)} IBANs distintos: "
            f"{unique_ibans.tolist()}. "
            "PROFIN espera un fichero de una única cuenta."
        )
    detected_iban = str(unique_ibans[0]).strip()

    # V-08 — Zero amounts: warning only
    zero_amounts = df[df["amount"] == 0.0]
    if not zero_amounts.empty:
        warnings.append(ValidationWarning(
            rule="V-08",
            message=(
                f"{len(zero_amounts)} fila(s) con importe = 0,00 €. "
                "Se importarán pero pueden indicar ajustes contables."
            ),
            row_indices=zero_amounts.index.tolist(),
        ))

    return ValidationResult(
        row_count=len(df),
        iban=detected_iban,
        warnings=warnings,
    )
```

## `app/ingestion/duplicate_detector.py`

```python
"""
app/ingestion/duplicate_detector.py
=====================================
PROFIN — Duplicate Detection Logic

Duplicate detection priority rules (from spec):
  D-1  EXACT match on (transaction_date, concepto_comun, amount,
       running_balance) → near-certain duplicate, auto-skip.
       Logged with is_duplicate_flagged=True, duplicate_resolved_by_user=True.

  D-2  Match on (transaction_date, concepto_comun, amount) but DIFFERENT
       running_balance → likely a genuine repeated transaction
       (e.g., two identical payments same day). Do NOT auto-skip.
       Imported as new, NOT flagged.

  D-3  Match on (transaction_date, concepto_comun, amount, running_balance)
       that is ambiguous per known patterns → flag is_duplicate_flagged=True,
       prompt user via GUI dialog. User resolves Yes/No.
       NOTE: In v1 with the 4-field exact match in D-1, D-3 is only triggered
       for edge cases where the match is ambiguous despite exact field match
       (e.g., same-day batch transfers). Implementing as a flag list that
       the import flow GUI resolves interactively.

  D-4  Never silently guess on ambiguous cases — always defer to user.

Output:
  DuplicateCheckResult.auto_skip    : rows to skip (D-1 certain duplicates)
  DuplicateCheckResult.needs_review : rows needing user confirmation (D-3)
  DuplicateCheckResult.clean        : rows safe to import without question
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date
from typing import List, Set, Tuple

import pandas as pd
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.models import Transaction


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Result type
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class DuplicateCheckResult:
    """
    Result of running duplicate detection on an incoming DataFrame.

    Attributes
    ----------
    auto_skip : List[int]
        DataFrame row indices that are certain duplicates (D-1).
        These are SKIPPED automatically — no user prompt.
    needs_review : List[int]
        DataFrame row indices that are possible/ambiguous duplicates (D-3).
        GUI must prompt the user for each.
    clean : List[int]
        DataFrame row indices that are clean — safe to import.
    skip_reasons : dict[int, str]
        Human-readable reason string for each auto_skip index.
    review_reasons : dict[int, str]
        Human-readable reason string for each needs_review index.
    """
    auto_skip:      List[int] = field(default_factory=list)
    needs_review:   List[int] = field(default_factory=list)
    clean:          List[int] = field(default_factory=list)
    skip_reasons:   dict      = field(default_factory=dict)
    review_reasons: dict      = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def total_incoming(self) -> int:
        return len(self.auto_skip) + len(self.needs_review) + len(self.clean)

    @property
    def summary(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"Total: {self.total_incoming} | "
            f"Limpios: {len(self.clean)} | "
            f"Auto-omitidos: {len(self.auto_skip)} | "
            f"Revisión pendiente: {len(self.needs_review)}"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def check_duplicates(
    incoming: pd.DataFrame,
    session: Session,
    user_id: int,
    account_id: int,
) -> DuplicateCheckResult:
    """
    Check each row of `incoming` against existing transactions in the DB.

    Parameters
    ----------
    incoming : pd.DataFrame
        Output of excel_parser.parse_excel() (already validated).
    session : Session
        Active SQLAlchemy session (read-only during this call).
    user_id : int
        ID of the authenticated user.
    account_id : int
        ID of the target account.

    Returns
    -------
    DuplicateCheckResult
    """
    result = DuplicateCheckResult()

    # -- Build lookup set from existing transactions ----------------------
    # Key for D-1 exact match: (date, concepto_comun, amount, running_balance)
    # Key for D-2/D-3 partial: (date, concepto_comun, amount)
    existing_exact: Set[Tuple] = set()
    existing_partial: Set[Tuple] = set()

    existing_txns = (
        session.query(
            Transaction.transaction_date,
            Transaction.concepto_comun,
            Transaction.amount,
            Transaction.running_balance,
        )
        .filter(
            Transaction.user_id    == user_id,
            Transaction.account_id == account_id,
        )
        .all()
    )

    for txn in existing_txns:
        exact_key   = (txn.transaction_date, txn.concepto_comun,
                       txn.amount, txn.running_balance)
        partial_key = (txn.transaction_date, txn.concepto_comun, txn.amount)
        existing_exact.add(exact_key)
        existing_partial.add(partial_key)

    # -- Check each incoming row -----------------------------------------
    # Track duplicates WITHIN the incoming batch itself (intra-batch)
    # to catch duplicate rows inside the same file.
    intra_batch_exact: Set[Tuple]   = set()
    intra_batch_partial: Set[Tuple] = set()

    for idx, row in incoming.iterrows():
        txn_date    = row["transaction_date"]
        conc_comun  = row.get("concepto_comun")
        amount      = row["amount"]
        balance     = row["running_balance"]

        exact_key   = (txn_date, conc_comun, amount, balance)
        partial_key = (txn_date, conc_comun, amount)

        # D-1: Exact match against DB → auto-skip
        if exact_key in existing_exact:
            result.auto_skip.append(idx)
            result.skip_reasons[idx] = (
                f"Duplicado exacto en BD: "
                f"{txn_date} | {conc_comun} | {amount:+.2f} € | "
                f"Saldo {balance:.2f} €"
            )
            continue

        # D-1 intra-batch: exact match within this same file → auto-skip
        if exact_key in intra_batch_exact:
            result.auto_skip.append(idx)
            result.skip_reasons[idx] = (
                f"Duplicado exacto dentro del fichero: "
                f"{txn_date} | {conc_comun} | {amount:+.2f} € | "
                f"Saldo {balance:.2f} €"
            )
            continue

        # D-2: Partial match (same date + concepto + amount, diff balance)
        # → This is a genuine repeated transaction. Import as clean, no flag.
        # (We do NOT add a needs_review here per spec rule D-2.)

        # D-2: Partial match (same date + concepto + amount, diff balance)
        # → This is a genuine repeated transaction and should be imported
        # as a normal row (clean). No user flagging for intra-batch partial
        # matches per spec. We therefore fall through to the 'clean' branch.

        # Clean row
        result.clean.append(idx)
        intra_batch_exact.add(exact_key)
        intra_batch_partial.add(partial_key)

    return result
```

## `app/ingestion/importer.py`

```python
"""
app/ingestion/importer.py
==========================
PROFIN — Import Orchestrator

Responsibility:
  Orchestrate the full import pipeline:
    1. Parse   : excel_parser.parse_excel()
    2. Validate: validator.validate_dataframe()
    3. Duplicate check: duplicate_detector.check_duplicates()
    4. Persist : write ImportBatch + Transaction rows to DB
    5. Integrity check: trigger balance reconciliation after import

  This module contains NO GUI logic — it returns structured results
  that the GUI (app/gui/import_flow.py) uses to drive dialogs and progress.

Key design decisions:
  - All DB writes happen inside a single transaction per batch.
    On any unhandled exception, the session is rolled back cleanly.
  - User-resolved duplicate decisions are passed in as a dict
    {row_index: bool} where True = "import anyway", False = "skip".
  - TransactionType is derived from amount sign:
      amount > 0 → income
      amount < 0 → expense
      amount == 0 → unknown
  - source_file stores only the filename, not the full path (privacy).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

import pandas as pd
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.db.models import (
    Account,
    AuditAction,
    AuditLog,
    ImportBatch,
    ImportStatus,
    Transaction,
    TransactionType,
)
from app.ingestion.duplicate_detector import DuplicateCheckResult
from app.ingestion.excel_parser import parse_excel
from app.ingestion.validator import ValidationResult, validate_dataframe

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Result types
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class ImportPreview:
    """
    Returned after parse + validate + duplicate-check, BEFORE any DB writes.
    Used by the GUI to display the import preview dialog.
    """
    df:                pd.DataFrame
    validation_result: ValidationResult
    duplicate_result:  DuplicateCheckResult
    file_path:         Path

    @property
    def clean_count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.duplicate_result.clean)

    @property
    def auto_skip_count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.duplicate_result.auto_skip)

    @property
    def needs_review_count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.duplicate_result.needs_review)


@dataclass
class ImportSummary:
    """
    Returned after DB writes are complete.
    """
    import_batch_id:  int
    rows_imported:    int
    rows_skipped:     int
    rows_errors:      int
    warnings:         List[str]  = field(default_factory=list)
    imported_ids:     List[int]  = field(default_factory=list)
    status:           ImportStatus = ImportStatus.completed


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API — Stage 1: Preview (no DB writes)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def prepare_import(
    file_path:  Path,
    session:    Session,
    user_id:    int,
    account_id: int,
) -> ImportPreview:
    """
    Parse, validate, and check for duplicates WITHOUT writing to the DB.
    Returns an ImportPreview the GUI uses to show the import preview dialog.

    Parameters
    ----------
    file_path  : Path to the .xlsx file.
    session    : Active SQLAlchemy read session.
    user_id    : Authenticated user ID.
    account_id : Target account ID.

    Returns
    -------
    ImportPreview

    Raises
    ------
    FileNotFoundError   : If file does not exist.
    ValueError          : If Excel file is unreadable or malformed.
    ValidationError     : If any blocking validation rule fails.
    """
    df                = parse_excel(file_path)
    validation_result = validate_dataframe(df)
    duplicate_result  = _check_duplicates_safe(
        df, session, user_id, account_id
    )

    return ImportPreview(
        df=df,
        validation_result=validation_result,
        duplicate_result=duplicate_result,
        file_path=file_path,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public API — Stage 2: Commit (DB writes)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def commit_import(
    preview:           ImportPreview,
    session:           Session,
    user_id:           int,
    account_id:        int,
    user_decisions:    Dict[int, bool],
) -> ImportSummary:
    """
    Write the approved rows to the DB as a single atomic transaction.

    Parameters
    ----------
    preview        : ImportPreview from prepare_import().
    session        : Active SQLAlchemy session (will be committed here).
    user_id        : Authenticated user ID.
    account_id     : Target account ID.
    user_decisions : {row_index: True/False}
                     True  = user says "import this possible duplicate"
                     False = user says "skip this possible duplicate"
                     Rows in needs_review that are NOT in this dict are
                     skipped by default (conservative).

    Returns
    -------
    ImportSummary
    """
    df               = preview.df
    dup_result       = preview.duplicate_result
    file_path        = preview.file_path
    warnings: List[str] = [w.message for w in preview.validation_result.warnings]

    rows_imported = 0
    rows_skipped  = 0
    rows_errors   = 0
    imported_ids: List[int] = []

    try:
        # -- Create ImportBatch record ------------------------------------
        batch = ImportBatch(
            user_id          = user_id,
            filename         = file_path.name,
            import_timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            rows_imported    = 0,
            rows_skipped     = 0,
            status           = ImportStatus.pending,
        )
        session.add(batch)
        session.flush()  # get batch.id before inserting transactions

        # -- Determine which rows to import ------------------------------
        # auto_skip → always skip
        # needs_review → import only if user_decisions[idx] is True
        # clean → always import
        skip_indices = set(dup_result.auto_skip)
        for idx in dup_result.needs_review:
            if not user_decisions.get(idx, False):
                skip_indices.add(idx)

        import_indices = [
            i for i in range(len(df))
            if i not in skip_indices
        ]

        # -- Insert transactions -----------------------------------------
        for idx in import_indices:
            row = df.iloc[idx]
            try:
                txn = _row_to_transaction(
                    row        = row,
                    user_id    = user_id,
                    account_id = account_id,
                    batch_id   = batch.id,
                    source_file= file_path.name,
                    is_flagged = idx in dup_result.needs_review,
                    resolved   = idx in dup_result.needs_review,
                )
                session.add(txn)
                session.flush()   # get txn.id immediately
                imported_ids.append(txn.id)
                rows_imported += 1

                # Audit log entry
                session.add(AuditLog(
                    user_id       = user_id,
                    table_affected= "transactions",
                    record_id     = None,  # filled after flush
                    action        = AuditAction.insert,
                    old_value     = None,
                    new_value     = (
                        f"{row['transaction_date']} | "
                        f"{row.get('issuer_name','')} | "
                        f"{row['amount']:+.2f}"
                    ),
                    timestamp     = datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                ))

            except Exception as row_exc:
                logger.warning(
                    "Error al insertar fila %d: %s", idx, row_exc
                )
                rows_errors += 1
                warnings.append(
                    f"Fila {idx}: error al insertar — {row_exc}"
                )

        rows_skipped = len(skip_indices)

        # -- Update batch counters & status ------------------------------
        batch.rows_imported = rows_imported
        batch.rows_skipped  = rows_skipped
        batch.status = (
            ImportStatus.completed
            if rows_errors == 0
            else ImportStatus.partial
        )

        session.commit()
        logger.info(
            "Import completado: batch_id=%d, importadas=%d, "
            "omitidas=%d, errores=%d",
            batch.id, rows_imported, rows_skipped, rows_errors,
        )

        return ImportSummary(
            import_batch_id = batch.id,
            rows_imported   = rows_imported,
            rows_skipped    = rows_skipped,
            rows_errors     = rows_errors,
            warnings        = warnings,
            imported_ids    = imported_ids,
            status          = batch.status,
        )

    except Exception as exc:
        session.rollback()
        logger.error("Import abortado, rollback ejecutado: %s", exc)
        raise


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Internal helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _check_duplicates_safe(
    df:         pd.DataFrame,
    session:    Session,
    user_id:    int,
    account_id: int,
):
    """Wrap duplicate_detector import to avoid circular import at module level."""
    from app.ingestion.duplicate_detector import check_duplicates
    return check_duplicates(df, session, user_id, account_id)


def _derive_type(amount: float) -> TransactionType:
    """
    Derive TransactionType from the signed amount.
    Investment type cannot be derived from amount alone — it requires
    manual or rule-based categorisation. amount > 0 = income,
    amount < 0 = expense, 0 = unknown.
    """
    if amount > 0:
        return TransactionType.income
    elif amount < 0:
        return TransactionType.expense
    return TransactionType.unknown


def _row_to_transaction(
    row:         pd.Series,
    user_id:     int,
    account_id:  int,
    batch_id:    int,
    source_file: str,
    is_flagged:  bool,
    resolved:    bool,
) -> Transaction:
    """Map one DataFrame row to a Transaction ORM object."""
    return Transaction(
        user_id                    = user_id,
        account_id                 = account_id,
        import_batch_id            = batch_id,
        transaction_date           = row["transaction_date"],
        value_date                 = row.get("value_date"),
        amount                     = float(row["amount"]),
        running_balance            = float(row["running_balance"]),
        type                       = _derive_type(float(row["amount"])),
        concepto_comun             = row.get("concepto_comun"),
        concepto_propio            = row.get("concepto_propio"),
        issuer_name                = row.get("issuer_name"),
        concept                    = row.get("concept"),
        raw_col14                  = row.get("raw_col14"),
        source_file                = source_file,
        is_duplicate_flagged       = is_flagged,
        duplicate_resolved_by_user = resolved,
        category_id                = None,  # assigned by rules engine later
    )
```
