```python
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel#panelTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#selectedInfo {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QFrame#panelSep {{
                color: {t["border"]};
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                max-height: 1px;
            }}
        """)
```

## `app/gui/import_view.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/import_view.py
=======================
PROFIN — Import View (Fase 4b)

Flow:
  Step 1 — File selection  : Browse for .xlsx file
  Step 2 — Preview         : Table showing new vs duplicate rows
                             + duplicate resolution dialogs
  Step 3 — Confirm         : Commit to DB, show import summary

Design decisions:
  - Uses prepare_import() (no DB writes) for preview
  - Uses commit_import() for final DB writes
  - Preview table columns: Fecha | Emisor | Concepto | Importe | Tipo | Saldo
  - Duplicates flagged in orange; user resolves via inline checkbox
  - Auto-categorization runs after commit
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from typing import Optional, Dict, List

from PySide6.QtCore    import Qt, QThread, Signal, QObject
from PySide6.QtGui     import QFont, QColor
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QPushButton, QFrame, QFileDialog, QTableWidget,
    QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView, QProgressBar,
    QMessageBox, QSizePolicy, QStackedWidget,
    QAbstractItemView, QCheckBox, QScrollArea,
)
import qtawesome as qta

from app.config      import THEMES
from app.db.models   import User, Account, TransactionType
from sqlalchemy.orm  import Session


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Background worker for parse + validate (keeps GUI responsive)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _ParseWorker(QObject):
    finished = Signal(object)   # ImportPreview or Exception
    error    = Signal(str)

    def __init__(self, file_path: str, session: Session,
                 user_id: int, account_id: int) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._file_path  = file_path
        self._session    = session
        self._user_id    = user_id
        self._account_id = account_id

    def run(self) -> None:
        try:
            from app.ingestion.importer import prepare_import
            preview = prepare_import(
                file_path  = self._file_path,
                session    = self._session,
                user_id    = self._user_id,
                account_id = self._account_id,
            )
            self.finished.emit(preview)
        except Exception as exc:
            self.error.emit(str(exc))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main Import View
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class ImportView(QWidget):
    """Three-step import wizard embedded in the main stacked view."""

    STEP_SELECT  = 0
    STEP_PREVIEW = 1
    STEP_SUMMARY = 2

    def __init__(self, user: User, session: Session,
                 theme: str = "light") -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._user     = user
        self._session  = session
        self._theme    = theme
        self._t        = THEMES[theme]
        self._preview  = None   # ImportPreview object
        self._decisions: Dict[int, bool] = {}  # row_idx -> import (True) / skip (False)
        self._account_id: Optional[int] = None

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_theme()
        self._ensure_account()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UI construction
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(0)

        # Top bar
        topbar = self._make_topbar()
        root.addWidget(topbar)

        # Step indicator
        self._step_bar = self._make_step_bar()
        root.addWidget(self._step_bar)

        # Stacked steps
        self._stack = QStackedWidget()
        root.addWidget(self._stack)

        self._stack.addWidget(self._make_step_select())
        self._stack.addWidget(self._make_step_preview())
        self._stack.addWidget(self._make_step_summary())

        self._go_to_step(self.STEP_SELECT)

    def _make_topbar(self) -> QFrame:
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setObjectName("dashTopBar")
        bar.setFixedHeight(56)
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)

        lbl = QLabel("Importar movimientos")
        lbl.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 15, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        lbl.setObjectName("dashTitle")
        layout.addWidget(lbl)
        layout.addStretch()
        return bar

    def _make_step_bar(self) -> QFrame:
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setFixedHeight(48)
        bar.setObjectName("stepBar")
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(0)

        steps = ["1  Seleccionar fichero",
                 "2  Previsualizar",
                 "3  Resultado"]
        self._step_labels: List[QLabel] = []
        for i, text in enumerate(steps):
            lbl = QLabel(text)
            lbl.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            lbl.setObjectName(f"step_{i}")
            lbl.setFixedHeight(48)
            lbl.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                              QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
            layout.addWidget(lbl)
            self._step_labels.append(lbl)

            if i < len(steps) - 1:
                arrow = QLabel("›")
                arrow.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
                arrow.setFixedWidth(20)
                arrow.setObjectName("stepArrow")
                layout.addWidget(arrow)

        return bar

    def _make_step_select(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.setSpacing(20)
        layout.setContentsMargins(48, 48, 48, 48)

        # Drop zone
        self._drop_zone = QFrame()
        self._drop_zone.setObjectName("dropZone")
        self._drop_zone.setFixedSize(480, 220)
        dz_layout = QVBoxLayout(self._drop_zone)
        dz_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        dz_layout.setSpacing(12)

        lbl_icon = QLabel()
        lbl_icon.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_icon.setPixmap(
            qta.icon("mdi.file-excel-outline",
                     color=self._t["accent"]).pixmap(56, 56)
        )
        dz_layout.addWidget(lbl_icon)

        lbl_drop = QLabel("Selecciona el fichero de CaixaBank (.xls / .xlsx)")
        lbl_drop.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_drop.setObjectName("dropZoneText")
        dz_layout.addWidget(lbl_drop)

        lbl_hint = QLabel("Formato: export CaixaBank — 24 columnas sin cabecera")
        lbl_hint.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_hint.setObjectName("dropZoneHint")
        dz_layout.addWidget(lbl_hint)

        dz_layout.addSpacing(8)

        self._btn_browse = QPushButton(
            "  Buscar fichero..."
        )
        self._btn_browse.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.folder-open-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        self._btn_browse.setFixedHeight(40)
        self._btn_browse.setFixedWidth(200)
        self._btn_browse.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        self._btn_browse.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_browse.clicked.connect(self._on_browse)
        dz_layout.addWidget(self._btn_browse,
                            alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        layout.addWidget(self._drop_zone)

        # Selected file label
        self._lbl_selected = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_selected.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self._lbl_selected.setObjectName("selectedFile")
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_selected)

        # Progress bar (hidden by default)
        self._progress = QProgressBar()
        self._progress.setFixedWidth(480)
        self._progress.setFixedHeight(6)
        self._progress.setTextVisible(False)
        self._progress.setRange(0, 0)   # indeterminate
        self._progress.setVisible(False)
        self._progress.setObjectName("importProgress")
        layout.addWidget(self._progress,
                         alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        # Parse button
        self._btn_parse = QPushButton("  Analizar fichero")
        self._btn_parse.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.magnify", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        self._btn_parse.setFixedHeight(44)
        self._btn_parse.setFixedWidth(200)
        self._btn_parse.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        self._btn_parse.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_parse.setEnabled(False)
        self._btn_parse.clicked.connect(self._on_parse)
        layout.addWidget(self._btn_parse,
                         alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        self._selected_path: Optional[str] = None
        return w

    def _make_step_preview(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 16, 24, 16)
        layout.setSpacing(12)

        # Summary bar
        self._preview_summary = QFrame()
        self._preview_summary.setObjectName("previewSummary")
        self._preview_summary.setFixedHeight(52)
        sum_layout = QHBoxLayout(self._preview_summary)
        sum_layout.setContentsMargins(16, 0, 16, 0)
        sum_layout.setSpacing(24)

        self._lbl_total = self._make_summary_chip(
            "mdi.database-import-outline", "0 filas", "accent"
        )
        self._lbl_new = self._make_summary_chip(
            "mdi.plus-circle-outline", "0 nuevas", "income"
        )
        self._lbl_dups = self._make_summary_chip(
            "mdi.content-copy", "0 duplicados", "warning"
        )
        self._lbl_skip = self._make_summary_chip(
            "mdi.skip-next-outline", "0 omitidas", "neutral"
        )
        for chip in [self._lbl_total, self._lbl_new,
                     self._lbl_dups, self._lbl_skip]:
            sum_layout.addWidget(chip)
        sum_layout.addStretch()

        layout.addWidget(self._preview_summary)

        # Transaction table
        self._tbl_preview = QTableWidget()
        self._tbl_preview.setColumnCount(7)
        self._tbl_preview.setHorizontalHeaderLabels([
            "Fecha", "Emisor", "Concepto",
            "Importe", "Tipo", "Saldo", "Importar"
        ])
        self._tbl_preview.setEditTriggers(
            QAbstractItemView.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers
        )
        self._tbl_preview.setSelectionBehavior(
            QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows
        )
        self._tbl_preview.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self._tbl_preview.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self._tbl_preview.setShowGrid(False)

        hdr = self._tbl_preview.horizontalHeader()
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(4, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(5, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(6, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Fixed)
        self._tbl_preview.setColumnWidth(6, 80)

        layout.addWidget(self._tbl_preview)

        # Bottom button row
        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_row.setSpacing(12)

        self._btn_back_select = QPushButton("  Volver")
        self._btn_back_select.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.arrow-left", color=self._t["text_secondary"])
        )
        self._btn_back_select.setFixedHeight(40)
        self._btn_back_select.setObjectName("btnSecondary")
        self._btn_back_select.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_back_select.clicked.connect(
            lambda: self._go_to_step(self.STEP_SELECT)
        )

        self._btn_commit = QPushButton("  Confirmar importación")
        self._btn_commit.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.database-check-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        self._btn_commit.setFixedHeight(40)
        self._btn_commit.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        self._btn_commit.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_commit.clicked.connect(self._on_commit)

        btn_row.addWidget(self._btn_back_select)
        btn_row.addStretch()
        btn_row.addWidget(self._btn_commit)
        layout.addLayout(btn_row)

        return w

    def _make_step_summary(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.setSpacing(20)
        layout.setContentsMargins(48, 48, 48, 48)

        self._lbl_summary_icon = QLabel()
        self._lbl_summary_icon.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_summary_icon)

        self._lbl_summary_title = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_summary_title.setFont(
            QFont("Segoe UI", 18, QFont.Weight.Bold)
        )
        self._lbl_summary_title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_summary_title)

        self._lbl_summary_detail = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_summary_detail.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self._lbl_summary_detail.setObjectName("summaryDetail")
        self._lbl_summary_detail.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(self._lbl_summary_detail)

        btn_new = QPushButton("  Nueva importación")
        btn_new.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.upload-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        btn_new.setFixedHeight(44)
        btn_new.setFixedWidth(220)
        btn_new.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        btn_new.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_new.clicked.connect(
            lambda: self._go_to_step(self.STEP_SELECT)
        )
        layout.addWidget(btn_new, alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        return w

    def _make_summary_chip(self, icon_name: str,
                            text: str, color_key: str) -> QLabel:
        lbl = QLabel(f"  {text}")
        lbl.setObjectName(f"chip_{color_key}")
        return lbl

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Step navigation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _go_to_step(self, step: int) -> None:
        self._stack.setCurrentIndex(step)
        t = self._t
        for i, lbl in enumerate(self._step_labels):
            if i == step:
                lbl.setStyleSheet(
                    f"color: {t['accent']}; font-weight: 700;"
                    f"border-bottom: 2px solid {t['accent']};"
                )
            else:
                lbl.setStyleSheet(
                    f"color: {t['text_secondary']}; font-weight: 400;"
                    "border-bottom: none;"
                )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Slots — Step 1
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _on_browse(self) -> None:
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "Seleccionar fichero CaixaBank",
            os.path.expanduser("~"),
            "Ficheros CaixaBank (*.xls *.xlsx);;Todos los ficheros (*)"
        )
        if path:
            self._selected_path = path
            fname = os.path.basename(path)
            self._lbl_selected.setText(f"📄  {fname}")
            self._btn_parse.setEnabled(True)

    def _on_parse(self) -> None:
        if not self._selected_path:
            return
        if self._account_id is None:
            QMessageBox.warning(
                self, "Sin cuenta",
                "No se encontró ninguna cuenta bancaria. "
                "Por favor crea una cuenta antes de importar."
            )
            return

        self._btn_parse.setEnabled(False)
        self._btn_browse.setEnabled(False)
        self._progress.setVisible(True)

        # Run in background thread
        self._thread = QThread()
        from pathlib import Path
        self._worker = _ParseWorker(
            Path(self._selected_path), self._session,
            self._user.id, self._account_id
        )
        self._worker.moveToThread(self._thread)
        self._thread.started.connect(self._worker.run)
        self._worker.finished.connect(self._on_parse_done)
        self._worker.error.connect(self._on_parse_error)
        self._worker.finished.connect(self._thread.quit)
        self._worker.error.connect(self._thread.quit)
        self._thread.start()

    def _on_parse_done(self, preview) -> None:
        self._progress.setVisible(False)
        self._btn_parse.setEnabled(True)
        self._btn_browse.setEnabled(True)
        self._preview = preview
        self._populate_preview_table(preview)
        self._go_to_step(self.STEP_PREVIEW)

    def _on_parse_error(self, error_msg: str) -> None:
        self._progress.setVisible(False)
        self._btn_parse.setEnabled(True)
        self._btn_browse.setEnabled(True)
        QMessageBox.critical(
            self, "Error de validación", error_msg
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Preview table population
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _populate_preview_table(self, preview) -> None:
        """
        Populate the preview table from an ImportPreview object.

        ImportPreview structure (real):
          preview.df                  → pd.DataFrame (all rows)
          preview.duplicate_result    → DuplicateCheckResult
            .clean        → List[int]  row indices safe to import
            .auto_skip    → List[int]  certain duplicates (auto-skipped)
            .needs_review → List[int]  possible duplicates (user decides)
            .review_reasons → dict[int, str]
        """
        t          = self._t
        df         = preview.df
        dup_result = preview.duplicate_result

        n_total    = len(df)
        n_skip     = len(dup_result.auto_skip)
        n_review   = len(dup_result.needs_review)
        n_clean    = len(dup_result.clean)

        self._lbl_total.setText(f"  {n_total} filas")
        self._lbl_new.setText(f"  {n_clean} nuevas")
        self._lbl_dups.setText(f"  {n_review} posibles duplicados")
        self._lbl_skip.setText(f"  {n_skip} omitidas (exactas)")

        # Only show clean + needs_review rows (auto_skip are hidden)
        visible_indices = sorted(dup_result.clean + dup_result.needs_review)

        self._tbl_preview.setRowCount(len(visible_indices))
        self._decisions = {}

        type_labels = {
            TransactionType.income:     "Ingreso",
            TransactionType.expense:    "Gasto",
            TransactionType.investment: "Inversión",
        }

        for tbl_row, df_idx in enumerate(visible_indices):
            row       = df.iloc[df_idx]
            is_review = df_idx in dup_result.needs_review

            # -- Fecha
            d = row.get("transaction_date", "")
            item_date = QTableWidgetItem(str(d) if d else "")

            # -- Emisor
            item_issuer = QTableWidgetItem(
                str(row.get("issuer_name", "") or "")[:40]
            )

            # -- Concepto
            item_concept = QTableWidgetItem(
                str(row.get("concept", "") or "")[:80]
            )

            # -- Importe
            amount = float(row.get("amount", 0.0) or 0.0)
            item_amount = QTableWidgetItem(self._fmt_amount(amount))
            item_amount.setTextAlignment(
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight |
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
            )
            item_amount.setForeground(
                QColor(t["income"] if amount >= 0 else t["expense"])
            )

            # -- Tipo (derived from amount sign)
            if amount > 0:
                txn_type = TransactionType.income
            elif amount < 0:
                txn_type = TransactionType.expense
            else:
                txn_type = None
            item_type = QTableWidgetItem(
                type_labels.get(txn_type, "—")
            )

            # -- Saldo
            balance = float(row.get("running_balance", 0.0) or 0.0)
            item_balance = QTableWidgetItem(self._fmt_amount(balance))
            item_balance.setTextAlignment(
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight |
                Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
            )

            # -- Apply row styling
            for col, item in enumerate([
                item_date, item_issuer, item_concept,
                item_amount, item_type, item_balance
            ]):
                item.setFlags(
                    Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEnabled |
                    Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable
                )
                if is_review:
                    # Orange tint for possible duplicates
                    item.setBackground(QColor(t["warning"] + "30"))
                    item.setToolTip(
                        dup_result.review_reasons.get(df_idx, "Posible duplicado")
                    )
                self._tbl_preview.setItem(tbl_row, col, item)

            # -- Checkbox col 6: clean=checked, needs_review=unchecked
            chk = QCheckBox()
            chk.setChecked(not is_review)
            chk.setStyleSheet("margin-left: 24px;")
            # Store decision keyed by df_idx (not tbl_row) for commit_import
            self._decisions[df_idx] = not is_review
            chk.stateChanged.connect(
                lambda state, idx=df_idx: self._decisions.__setitem__(
                    idx, state == Qt.CheckState.Checked.value
                )
            )
            self._tbl_preview.setCellWidget(tbl_row, 6, chk)

        self._tbl_preview.resizeRowsToContents()
        import logging
        logging.getLogger(__name__).info(
            "Preview: %d filas visibles (%d limpias, %d revisión, %d omitidas)",
            len(visible_indices), n_clean, n_review, n_skip,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Slots — Step 2 (Commit)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _on_commit(self) -> None:
        if self._preview is None:
            return

        to_import = sum(1 for v in self._decisions.values() if v)
        if to_import == 0:
            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "Sin filas seleccionadas",
                "No hay filas marcadas para importar. "
                "Selecciona al menos una fila."
            )
            return

        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self,
            "Confirmar importación",
            f"Se importarán <b>{to_import}</b> transacciones "
            f"a la base de datos.<br><br>¿Continuar?",
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes |
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.No,
        )
        if reply != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
            return

        try:
            from app.ingestion.importer import commit_import
            from app.categorization.rules_engine import apply_rules_and_commit

            result = commit_import(
                preview        = self._preview,
                session        = self._session,
                user_id        = self._user.id,
                account_id     = self._account_id,
                user_decisions = self._decisions,
            )

            # Auto-categorize imported transactions
            if result.imported_ids:
                rules_result = apply_rules_and_commit(
                    self._session,
                    result.imported_ids,
                    self._user.id,
                )
                auto_cat = len(rules_result.assigned)
            else:
                auto_cat = 0

            self._show_summary(
                success     = True,
                imported    = result.rows_imported,
                skipped     = result.rows_skipped,
                auto_cat    = auto_cat,
            )

        except Exception as exc:
            self._show_summary(success=False, error=str(exc))

    def _show_summary(self, success: bool,
                       imported: int = 0, skipped: int = 0,
                       auto_cat: int = 0,
                       error: str = "") -> None:
        t = self._t
        if success:
            self._lbl_summary_icon.setPixmap(
                qta.icon("mdi.check-circle-outline",
                         color=t["income"]).pixmap(72, 72)
            )
            self._lbl_summary_title.setText("¡Importación completada!")
            self._lbl_summary_title.setStyleSheet(
                f"color: {t['income']}; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700;"
            )
            self._lbl_summary_detail.setText(
                f"<b>{imported}</b> transacciones importadas  ·  "
                f"<b>{skipped}</b> omitidas  ·  "
                f"<b>{auto_cat}</b> auto-categorizadas"
            )
        else:
            self._lbl_summary_icon.setPixmap(
                qta.icon("mdi.alert-circle-outline",
                         color=t["expense"]).pixmap(72, 72)
            )
            self._lbl_summary_title.setText("Error en la importación")
            self._lbl_summary_title.setStyleSheet(
                f"color: {t['expense']}; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700;"
            )
            self._lbl_summary_detail.setText(error)

        self._go_to_step(self.STEP_SUMMARY)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _ensure_account(self) -> None:
        """Get or create default account for this user."""
        account = (
            self._session.query(Account)
            .filter(Account.user_id == self._user.id)
            .first()
        )
        if account:
            self._account_id = account.id
        else:
            # Create default account on first import
            acct = Account(
                user_id      = self._user.id,
                account_name = "CaixaBank Principal",
                iban_last4   = "0000",
            )
            self._session.add(acct)
            self._session.commit()
            self._session.refresh(acct)
            self._account_id = acct.id

    @staticmethod
    def _fmt_amount(value: float) -> str:
        sign = "+" if value >= 0 else ""
        return (
            f"{sign}{abs(value):,.2f} €"
            .replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")
        )

    def _apply_theme(self) -> None:
        t = self._t
        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#dashTopBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#dashTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QFrame#stepBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QFrame#dropZone {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 2px dashed {t["border_strong"]};
                border-radius: 16px;
            }}
            QLabel#dropZoneText {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600;
            }}
            QLabel#dropZoneHint {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel#selectedFile {{
                color: {t["accent"]};
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]};
                color: #FFFFFF; border: none;
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary:hover {{
                background-color: {t["accent_hover"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary:disabled {{
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary {{
                background-color: transparent;
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
            }}
            QTableWidget {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                gridline-color: transparent;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QTableWidget::item {{
                padding: 6px 8px;
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QTableWidget::item:selected {{
                background-color: {t["accent_light"]};
                color: {t["accent"]};
            }}
            QHeaderView::section {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700;
                padding: 6px 8px;
                border: none;
                border-bottom: 2px solid {t["border"]};
                text-transform: uppercase;
                letter-spacing: 0.5px;
            }}
            QTableWidget::item:alternate {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
            }}
            QFrame#previewSummary {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
            }}
            QProgressBar#importProgress {{
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 3px;
            }}
            QProgressBar#importProgress::chunk {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]};
                border-radius: 3px;
            }}
            QLabel#summaryDetail {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 14px;
            }}
        """)
```

## `app/gui/login_window.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/login_window.py
========================
PROFIN — Login Window

Features:
  - Fullscreen on launch (showMaximized)
  - Auto-fills last logged-in username from QSettings
  - Password field always empty (security)
  - Light/dark theme toggle (persisted)
  - Modern card-based layout centred on screen
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Optional

from PySide6.QtCore    import Qt, QSettings
from PySide6.QtGui     import QFont, QColor, QPalette, QIcon
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QLineEdit, QPushButton, QCheckBox, QFrame,
    QApplication, QMessageBox, QSizePolicy,
)
import qtawesome as qta

from app.config        import APP_NAME, APP_VERSION, THEMES
from app.db.database   import get_session
from app.auth.auth_service import verify_login
from app.settings      import SettingsManager


class LoginWindow(QWidget):
    """
    Full-screen login window.
    Emits no signals — on success it instantiates MainWindow and closes self.
    """

    SETTINGS_ORG  = "GEVernova"
    SETTINGS_APP  = "PROFIN"
    KEY_LAST_USER = "auth/last_username"
    KEY_THEME     = "ui/theme"

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._settings = QSettings(self.SETTINGS_ORG, self.SETTINGS_APP)
        saved_settings = SettingsManager().load()
        remember_theme = saved_settings.save_appearance_on_start
        startup_theme = self._settings.value(self.KEY_THEME, saved_settings.theme if remember_theme else "light")
        self._theme = startup_theme if startup_theme in THEMES else (saved_settings.theme if remember_theme else "light")
        if not remember_theme:
            self._settings.setValue(self.KEY_THEME, "light")
        self._session  = get_session()

        self.setWindowTitle(f"{APP_NAME}  v{APP_VERSION}")

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_theme()
        self._load_last_user()

        self.showMaximized()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UI construction
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(0)

        # ── Top bar ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        self._top_bar = QFrame()
        self._top_bar.setFixedHeight(48)
        top_layout = QHBoxLayout(self._top_bar)
        top_layout.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)

        lbl_brand = QLabel(f"<b>{APP_NAME}</b>")
        lbl_brand.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 13, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        top_layout.addWidget(lbl_brand)
        top_layout.addStretch()

        # Theme toggle
        self._btn_theme = QPushButton()
        self._btn_theme.setFixedSize(36, 36)
        self._btn_theme.setFlat(True)
        self._btn_theme.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_theme.setToolTip("Cambiar tema claro/oscuro")
        self._btn_theme.clicked.connect(self._toggle_theme)
        top_layout.addWidget(self._btn_theme)

        root.addWidget(self._top_bar)

        # ── Centre area ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        centre = QWidget()
        centre.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding,
                             QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding)
        centre_layout = QVBoxLayout(centre)
        centre_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        root.addWidget(centre)

        # Card
        self._card = QFrame()
        self._card.setFixedWidth(420)
        self._card.setObjectName("loginCard")
        card_layout = QVBoxLayout(self._card)
        card_layout.setContentsMargins(40, 40, 40, 40)
        card_layout.setSpacing(20)
        centre_layout.addWidget(self._card)

        # App icon + title
        lbl_icon = QLabel()
        lbl_icon.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        icon_pixmap = qta.icon(
            "mdi.chart-areaspline", color="#2563EB"
        ).pixmap(56, 56)
        lbl_icon.setPixmap(icon_pixmap)
        card_layout.addWidget(lbl_icon)

        lbl_title = QLabel(APP_NAME)
        lbl_title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_title.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 22, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        lbl_title.setObjectName("loginTitle")
        card_layout.addWidget(lbl_title)

        lbl_sub = QLabel("Personal & Project Finance Tracker")
        lbl_sub.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_sub.setObjectName("loginSubtitle")
        card_layout.addWidget(lbl_sub)

        card_layout.addSpacing(8)

        # Username
        lbl_user = QLabel("Usuario")
        lbl_user.setObjectName("fieldLabel")
        card_layout.addWidget(lbl_user)

        self._inp_user = QLineEdit()
        self._inp_user.setPlaceholderText("Nombre de usuario")
        self._inp_user.setFixedHeight(42)
        self._inp_user.setObjectName("loginInput")
        card_layout.addWidget(self._inp_user)

        # Password
        lbl_pass = QLabel("Contraseña")
        lbl_pass.setObjectName("fieldLabel")
        card_layout.addWidget(lbl_pass)

        self._inp_pass = QLineEdit()
        self._inp_pass.setPlaceholderText("Contraseña")
        self._inp_pass.setEchoMode(QLineEdit.EchoMode.Password)
        self._inp_pass.setFixedHeight(42)
        self._inp_pass.setObjectName("loginInput")
        self._inp_pass.returnPressed.connect(self._on_login)
        card_layout.addWidget(self._inp_pass)

        # Error label (hidden by default)
        self._lbl_error = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_error.setObjectName("loginError")
        self._lbl_error.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self._lbl_error.setVisible(False)
        card_layout.addWidget(self._lbl_error)

        # Login button
        self._btn_login = QPushButton("  Iniciar Sesión")
        self._btn_login.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.login", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        self._btn_login.setFixedHeight(44)
        self._btn_login.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        self._btn_login.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_login.clicked.connect(self._on_login)
        card_layout.addWidget(self._btn_login)

        # Divider
        div = QFrame()
        div.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
        div.setObjectName("loginDivider")
        card_layout.addWidget(div)

        # Register link
        self._btn_register = QPushButton("  ¿Primera vez? Crear cuenta")
        self._btn_register.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.account-plus-outline", color="#2563EB")
        )
        self._btn_register.setFixedHeight(38)
        self._btn_register.setObjectName("btnSecondary")
        self._btn_register.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self._btn_register.clicked.connect(self._on_register)
        card_layout.addWidget(self._btn_register)

        card_layout.addSpacing(8)

        # Version footer
        lbl_ver = QLabel(f"v{APP_VERSION}")
        lbl_ver.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_ver.setObjectName("loginVersion")
        card_layout.addWidget(lbl_ver)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Slots
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _on_login(self) -> None:
        username = self._inp_user.text().strip()
        password = self._inp_pass.text()

        if not username:
            self._show_error("Por favor introduce tu nombre de usuario.")
            return
        if not password:
            self._show_error("Por favor introduce tu contraseña.")
            return

        user = verify_login(self._session, username, password)

        if user is None:
            self._show_error("Usuario o contraseña incorrectos.")
            self._inp_pass.clear()
            self._inp_pass.setFocus()
            return

        # Persist last user
        self._settings.setValue(self.KEY_LAST_USER, username)
        self._settings.setValue(self.KEY_THEME,     self._theme)

        # Launch main window
        from app.gui.main_window import MainWindow
        self._main = MainWindow(user=user, session=self._session,
                                theme=self._theme)
        self._main.show()
        self.close()

    def _on_register(self) -> None:
        """Open the inline register dialog."""
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog, QDialogButtonBox

        t = THEMES[self._theme]

        dlg = QDialog(self)
        dlg.setWindowTitle("Crear nueva cuenta")
        dlg.setFixedWidth(360)
        dlg.setModal(True)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(dlg)
        layout.setContentsMargins(28, 28, 28, 28)
        layout.setSpacing(14)

        lbl_title = QLabel("Crear nueva cuenta")
        lbl_title.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 14, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        lbl_title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.addWidget(lbl_title)

        lbl_u = QLabel("Usuario")
        lbl_u.setObjectName("fieldLabel")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_u)

        inp_user = QLineEdit()
        inp_user.setPlaceholderText("Elige un nombre de usuario")
        inp_user.setFixedHeight(40)
        inp_user.setObjectName("loginInput")
        layout.addWidget(inp_user)

        lbl_p = QLabel("Contraseña")
        lbl_p.setObjectName("fieldLabel")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_p)

        inp_pass = QLineEdit()
        inp_pass.setPlaceholderText("Elige una contraseña")
        inp_pass.setEchoMode(QLineEdit.EchoMode.Password)
        inp_pass.setFixedHeight(40)
        inp_pass.setObjectName("loginInput")
        layout.addWidget(inp_pass)

        lbl_p2 = QLabel("Confirmar contraseña")
        lbl_p2.setObjectName("fieldLabel")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_p2)

        inp_pass2 = QLineEdit()
        inp_pass2.setPlaceholderText("Repite la contraseña")
        inp_pass2.setEchoMode(QLineEdit.EchoMode.Password)
        inp_pass2.setFixedHeight(40)
        inp_pass2.setObjectName("loginInput")
        layout.addWidget(inp_pass2)

        lbl_err = QLabel("")
        lbl_err.setObjectName("loginError")
        lbl_err.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_err.setVisible(False)
        layout.addWidget(lbl_err)

        btn_box = QDialogButtonBox()
        btn_create = btn_box.addButton(
            "Crear cuenta", QDialogButtonBox.ButtonRole.AcceptRole
        )
        btn_create.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        btn_create.setFixedHeight(40)
        btn_cancel = btn_box.addButton(
            "Cancelar", QDialogButtonBox.ButtonRole.RejectRole
        )
        btn_cancel.setObjectName("btnSecondary")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(40)
        layout.addWidget(btn_box)

        dlg.setStyleSheet(self.styleSheet())

        def _try_create() -> None:
            from app.auth.auth_service import create_user
            username = inp_user.text().strip()
            password = inp_pass.text()
            password2 = inp_pass2.text()

            if not username:
                lbl_err.setText("El nombre de usuario no puede estar vacío.")
                lbl_err.setVisible(True)
                return
            if not password:
                lbl_err.setText("La contraseña no puede estar vacía.")
                lbl_err.setVisible(True)
                return
            if password != password2:
                lbl_err.setText("Las contraseñas no coinciden.")
                lbl_err.setVisible(True)
                inp_pass2.clear()
                return
            if len(password) < 6:
                lbl_err.setText("La contraseña debe tener al menos 6 caracteres.")
                lbl_err.setVisible(True)
                return
            try:
                create_user(self._session, username, password)
                dlg.accept()
                # Auto-fill username after successful registration
                self._inp_user.setText(username)
                self._inp_pass.setFocus()
                self._show_error(
                    f"✓ Cuenta '{username}' creada. Introduce tu contraseña para entrar."
                )
                self._lbl_error.setStyleSheet(
                    f"color: {t['income']}; font-size: 12px;"
                )
            except ValueError as exc:
                lbl_err.setText(str(exc))
                lbl_err.setVisible(True)

        btn_box.accepted.connect(_try_create)
        btn_box.rejected.connect(dlg.reject)
        inp_pass2.returnPressed.connect(_try_create)

        dlg.exec()

    def _toggle_theme(self) -> None:
        self._theme = "dark" if self._theme == "light" else "light"
        self._apply_theme()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _show_error(self, msg: str) -> None:
        self._lbl_error.setText(msg)
        self._lbl_error.setVisible(True)

    def _load_last_user(self) -> None:
        last = self._settings.value(self.KEY_LAST_USER, "")
        if last:
            self._inp_user.setText(last)
            self._inp_pass.setFocus()
        else:
            self._inp_user.setFocus()

    def _apply_theme(self) -> None:
        t = THEMES[self._theme]
        is_dark = self._theme == "dark"

        # Theme button icon
        icon_name = "mdi.weather-night" if self._theme == "light" else "mdi.weather-sunny"
        self._btn_theme.setIcon(
            qta.icon(icon_name, color=t["text_primary"])
        )

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QWidget {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-family: "Segoe UI";
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QFrame#loginCard {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-radius: 16px;
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#loginTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 24px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QLabel#loginSubtitle {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel#fieldLabel {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 600;
                margin-bottom: 2px;
            }}
            QLineEdit#loginInput {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                border: 1.5px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                padding: 8px 12px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QLineEdit#loginInput:focus {{
                border: 1.5px solid {t["accent"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]};
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 14px;
                font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary:hover {{
                background-color: {t["accent_hover"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary:pressed {{
                background-color: {t["accent_hover"]};
            }}
            QLabel#loginError {{
                color: {t["expense"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel#loginVersion {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary {{
                background-color: transparent;
                color: {t["accent"]};
                border: 1.5px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: 500;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary:hover {{
                background-color: {t["accent_light"]};
                border-color: {t["accent"]};
            }}
            QFrame#loginDivider {{
                color: {t["border"]};
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                max-height: 1px;
                margin: 4px 0px;
            }}
            QFrame#topBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
        """)

        self._top_bar.setStyleSheet(
            f"background-color: {t['bg_secondary']};"
            f"border-bottom: 1px solid {t['border']};"
        )
```

## `app/gui/rules_view.py`

```python
"""
app/gui/rules_view.py
======================
PROFIN — Auto-Categorization Rules View (Fase 4b)

Features:
  - List of all rules (global + user) ordered by priority
  - Create new rule: pattern + match type + category + priority
  - Delete rule
  - Visual indicator for match type (contains/exact/regex)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import List

from PySide6.QtCore    import Qt
from PySide6.QtGui     import QFont, QColor, QStandardItemModel, QStandardItem
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QPushButton, QFrame, QTableView, QHeaderView,
    QDialog, QLineEdit, QComboBox, QSpinBox,
    QFormLayout, QDialogButtonBox, QMessageBox,
    QAbstractItemView, QSizePolicy,
)
import qtawesome as qta

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.config      import THEMES
from app.db.models   import (
    AutoCategorizationRule, MatchType, User, Category, CategoryLevel
)
from app.categorization.rules_engine  import (
    create_rule, delete_rule, get_all_rules, update_rule
)
from app.categorization.category_manager import get_all_categories


class RulesView(QWidget):

    def __init__(self, user: User, session: Session,
                 theme: str = "light") -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._user    = user
        self._session = session
        self._theme   = theme
        self._t       = THEMES[theme]
        self._rules:  List[AutoCategorizationRule] = []
        self._categories: List[Category] = []

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_theme()
        self._load_categories()
        self.refresh()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UI
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(0)

        root.addWidget(self._make_topbar())

        # Table
        self._table = QTableView()
        self._table.setSelectionBehavior(
            QAbstractItemView.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows
        )
        self._table.setEditTriggers(
            QAbstractItemView.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers
        )
        self._table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self._table.setShowGrid(False)
        self._table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)

        self._model = QStandardItemModel()
        self._model.setHorizontalHeaderLabels([
            "Prioridad", "Patrón", "Tipo match",
            "Categoría", "Ámbito"
        ])
        self._table.setModel(self._model)

        hdr = self._table.horizontalHeader()
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        hdr.setSectionResizeMode(4, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)

        root.addWidget(self._table)

        # Bottom bar
        bottom = QFrame()
        bottom.setObjectName("rulesBottom")
        bottom.setFixedHeight(52)
        bl = QHBoxLayout(bottom)
        bl.setContentsMargins(16, 8, 16, 8)
        bl.setSpacing(10)

        self._lbl_count = QLabel("")
        self._lbl_count.setObjectName("rulesCount")
        bl.addWidget(self._lbl_count)

        bl.addStretch()

        btn_edit = QPushButton("  Editar regla")
        btn_edit.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.pencil-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        btn_edit.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_edit.setObjectName("btnSecondary")
        btn_edit.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_edit.setToolTip("Editar la regla seleccionada")
        btn_edit.clicked.connect(self._on_edit_rule)
        bl.addWidget(btn_edit)

        btn_del = QPushButton("  Eliminar regla")
        btn_del.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.delete-outline", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        btn_del.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_del.setObjectName("btnDanger")
        btn_del.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_del.clicked.connect(self._on_delete)
        bl.addWidget(btn_del)

        btn_apply = QPushButton("  Aplicar reglas a transacciones")
        btn_apply.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.auto-fix", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        btn_apply.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_apply.setObjectName("btnAccent")
        btn_apply.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_apply.setToolTip(
            "Aplica todas las reglas activas a las transacciones sin categorizar"
        )
        btn_apply.clicked.connect(self._on_apply_rules)
        bl.addWidget(btn_apply)

        root.addWidget(bottom)

    def _make_topbar(self) -> QFrame:
        bar = QFrame()
        bar.setObjectName("dashTopBar")
        bar.setFixedHeight(56)
        layout = QHBoxLayout(bar)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 0, 24, 0)

        lbl = QLabel("Reglas de auto-categorización")
        lbl.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 15, QFont.Weight.Bold))
        lbl.setObjectName("dashTitle")
        layout.addWidget(lbl)

        layout.addStretch()

        btn_new = QPushButton("  Nueva regla")
        btn_new.setIcon(
            qta.icon("mdi.plus", color="#FFFFFF")
        )
        btn_new.setFixedHeight(34)
        btn_new.setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        btn_new.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_new.clicked.connect(self._on_new_rule)
        layout.addWidget(btn_new)

        return bar

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Data
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _load_categories(self) -> None:
        self._categories = get_all_categories(
            self._session, self._user.id, level=CategoryLevel.category
        )

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        self._rules = get_all_rules(self._session, user_id=self._user.id)
        self._model.removeRows(0, self._model.rowCount())
        t = self._t

        cat_map = {c.id: c.name for c in self._categories}
        match_labels = {
            MatchType.contains: "Contiene",
            MatchType.exact:    "Exacto",
            MatchType.regex:    "Regex",
        }
        match_colors = {
            MatchType.contains: t["accent"],
            MatchType.exact:    t["income"],
            MatchType.regex:    t["investment"],
        }

        for rule in self._rules:
            item_pri = QStandardItem(str(rule.priority))
            item_pri.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

            item_pat = QStandardItem(rule.match_pattern)

            item_mt = QStandardItem(
                match_labels.get(rule.match_type, str(rule.match_type))
            )
            item_mt.setForeground(
                QColor(match_colors.get(rule.match_type, t["text_primary"]))
            )
            item_mt.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            item_mt.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 11, QFont.Weight.Bold))

            cat_name = cat_map.get(rule.category_id, f"id={rule.category_id}")
            item_cat = QStandardItem(cat_name)

            scope = "Global" if rule.user_id is None else "Personal"
            item_scope = QStandardItem(scope)
            item_scope.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            if scope == "Global":
                item_scope.setForeground(QColor(t["investment"]))
            else:
                item_scope.setForeground(QColor(t["text_secondary"]))

            row = [item_pri, item_pat, item_mt, item_cat, item_scope]
            for item in row:
                item.setEditable(False)
            self._model.appendRow(row)

        self._lbl_count.setText(
            f"{len(self._rules)} regla(s) activa(s)"
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Slots
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _on_new_rule(self) -> None:
        # Always reload categories before opening the dialog
        # in case they were added in the Categories view after this view loaded
        self._load_categories()

        if not self._categories:
            QMessageBox.warning(
                self, "Sin categorías",
                "No hay categorías disponibles.\n\n"
                "Ve a la pantalla 'Categorías' → pulsa 'Cargar categorías "
                "por defecto' y vuelve aquí."
            )
            return

        t = self._t

        dlg = QDialog(self)
        dlg.setWindowTitle("Nueva regla de auto-categorización")
        dlg.setFixedWidth(440)
        dlg.setModal(True)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(dlg)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        layout.setSpacing(14)

        form = QFormLayout()
        form.setSpacing(10)

        # Pattern
        inp_pattern = QLineEdit()
        inp_pattern.setPlaceholderText("ej: MERCADONA, ^REPSOL, NOMINA PEDRO")
        inp_pattern.setFixedHeight(38)
        form.addRow("Patrón:", inp_pattern)

        # Match type
        cmb_type = QComboBox()
        cmb_type.setFixedHeight(38)
        cmb_type.addItem("Contiene (substring)", MatchType.contains)
        cmb_type.addItem("Exacto (full match)",  MatchType.exact)
        cmb_type.addItem("Regex (expresión regular)", MatchType.regex)
        form.addRow("Tipo de match:", cmb_type)

        # Category
        cmb_cat = QComboBox()
        cmb_cat.setFixedHeight(38)
        for cat in self._categories:
            cmb_cat.addItem(cat.name, cat.id)
        form.addRow("Categoría:", cmb_cat)

        # Priority
        spn_pri = QSpinBox()
        spn_pri.setRange(1, 999)
        spn_pri.setValue(100)
        spn_pri.setFixedHeight(38)
        form.addRow("Prioridad (1=mayor):", spn_pri)

        # Scope
        cmb_scope = QComboBox()
        cmb_scope.setFixedHeight(38)
        cmb_scope.addItem("Global (todos los usuarios)", None)
        cmb_scope.addItem("Personal (solo yo)",         self._user.id)
        form.addRow("Ámbito:", cmb_scope)

        layout.addLayout(form)

        # Hint
        lbl_hint = QLabel(
            "💡 Tip: usa <b>Contiene</b> para la mayoría de casos. "
            "Usa <b>Regex</b> para patrones avanzados."
        )
        lbl_hint.setWordWrap(True)
        lbl_hint.setStyleSheet(
            f"color: {t['text_secondary']}; font-size: 11px;"
        )
        layout.addWidget(lbl_hint)

        btn_box = QDialogButtonBox(
            QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok |
            QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Cancel
        )
        btn_box.button(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok).setText("Crear regla")
        btn_box.button(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok).setObjectName("btnPrimary")
        btn_box.accepted.connect(dlg.accept)
        btn_box.rejected.connect(dlg.reject)
        layout.addWidget(btn_box)

        dlg.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QDialog {{ background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]}; }}
            QLineEdit, QComboBox, QSpinBox {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                border: 1.5px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 4px 10px;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QLineEdit:focus, QComboBox:focus, QSpinBox:focus {{
                border-color: {t["accent"]};
            }}
            QLabel {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 13px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]}; color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none; border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
                padding: 6px 16px;
            }}
        """)

        if dlg.exec() != QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted:
            return

        pattern  = inp_pattern.text().strip()
        mt       = cmb_type.currentData()
        cat_id   = cmb_cat.currentData()
        priority = spn_pri.value()
        scope    = cmb_scope.currentData()

        if not pattern:
            QMessageBox.warning(
                self, "Patrón vacío",
                "El patrón no puede estar vacío."
            )
            return

        if cat_id is None:
            QMessageBox.warning(
                self, "Sin categoría",
                "Selecciona una categoría válida para la regla.\n\n"
                "Si no hay categorías disponibles, ve a la pantalla "
                "'Categorías' y carga las categorías por defecto primero."
            )
            return

        try:
            create_rule(
                self._session,
                category_id   = cat_id,
                match_pattern = pattern,
                match_type    = mt,
                priority      = priority,
                user_id       = scope,
            )
            self.refresh()
        except Exception as exc:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error al crear regla", str(exc))

    def _on_apply_rules(self) -> None:
        """
        Apply all active rules to every uncategorized transaction
        belonging to this user and refresh the rules count label.
        """
        import logging
        _log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

        try:
            from app.db.models import Transaction
            from app.categorization.rules_engine import apply_rules_and_commit

            # Get ALL uncategorized transaction IDs for this user
            # Re-process ALL transactions — overwrite=True ensures
            # that transactions already categorized by a previous
            # version of the rule are updated with the new category.
            all_ids = [
                row.id for row in (
                    self._session.query(Transaction.id)
                    .filter(Transaction.user_id == self._user.id)
                    .all()
                )
            ]

            if not all_ids:
                QMessageBox.information(
                    self,
                    "Sin transacciones",
                    "No hay transacciones en la base de datos.",
                )
                return

            result = apply_rules_and_commit(
                self._session,
                all_ids,
                self._user.id,
                overwrite=True,
            )

            n_assigned  = len(result.assigned)
            n_conflict  = len(result.conflicts)
            n_unmatched = len(result.unmatched)

            _log.info(
                "Reglas aplicadas: %d asignadas, %d conflictos, %d sin match",
                n_assigned, n_conflict, n_unmatched,
            )

            msg = (
                f"<b>{n_assigned}</b> transacciones categorizadas automáticamente."
            )
            if n_conflict:
                msg += (
                    f"<br><b>{n_conflict}</b> transacciones con conflicto de reglas "
                    f"(múltiples reglas coinciden) — requieren revisión manual."
                )
            if n_unmatched:
                msg += (
                    f"<br><b>{n_unmatched}</b> transacciones sin coincidencia "
                    f"— permanecen sin categorizar."
                )

            QMessageBox.information(self, "Reglas aplicadas", msg)

        except Exception as exc:
            QMessageBox.critical(
                self, "Error al aplicar reglas", str(exc)
            )

    def _on_edit_rule(self) -> None:
        """Open a pre-filled dialog to edit the selected rule."""
        idx = self._table.currentIndex()
        if not idx.isValid():
            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "Sin selección",
                "Selecciona una regla para editar."
            )
            return

        row_idx = idx.row()
        if row_idx >= len(self._rules):
            return

        rule = self._rules[row_idx]
        self._load_categories()

        if not self._categories:
            QMessageBox.warning(
                self, "Sin categorías",
                "No hay categorías disponibles para asignar."
            )
            return

        t = self._t

        dlg = QDialog(self)
        dlg.setWindowTitle(f"Editar regla — '{rule.match_pattern}'")
        dlg.setFixedWidth(440)
        dlg.setModal(True)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(dlg)
        layout.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        layout.setSpacing(14)

        form = QFormLayout()
        form.setSpacing(10)

        # Pattern — pre-filled
        inp_pattern = QLineEdit(rule.match_pattern)
        inp_pattern.setFixedHeight(38)
        form.addRow("Patrón:", inp_pattern)

        # Match type — pre-selected
        cmb_type = QComboBox()
        cmb_type.setFixedHeight(38)
        cmb_type.addItem("Contiene (substring)",      MatchType.contains)
        cmb_type.addItem("Exacto (full match)",        MatchType.exact)
        cmb_type.addItem("Regex (expresión regular)",  MatchType.regex)
        cmb_type.setCurrentIndex(
            [MatchType.contains, MatchType.exact, MatchType.regex]
            .index(rule.match_type)
        )
        form.addRow("Tipo de match:", cmb_type)

        # Category — pre-selected
        cmb_cat = QComboBox()
        cmb_cat.setFixedHeight(38)
        for cat in self._categories:
            cmb_cat.addItem(cat.name, cat.id)
        # Select current category
        for i in range(cmb_cat.count()):
            if cmb_cat.itemData(i) == rule.category_id:
                cmb_cat.setCurrentIndex(i)
                break
        form.addRow("Categoría:", cmb_cat)

        # Priority — pre-filled
        spn_pri = QSpinBox()
        spn_pri.setRange(1, 999)
        spn_pri.setValue(rule.priority)
        spn_pri.setFixedHeight(38)
        form.addRow("Prioridad (1=mayor):", spn_pri)

        # Scope — pre-selected (read-only info, scope cannot change)
        scope_label = "Global" if rule.user_id is None else "Personal"
        lbl_scope = QLabel(scope_label)
        lbl_scope.setFixedHeight(38)
        form.addRow("Ámbito:", lbl_scope)

        layout.addLayout(form)

        # Dialog buttons
        btns = QDialogButtonBox(
            QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Save |
            QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Cancel
        )
        btns.button(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Save).setText("  Guardar cambios")
        btns.button(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Cancel).setText("  Cancelar")
        btns.accepted.connect(dlg.accept)
        btns.rejected.connect(dlg.reject)
        layout.addWidget(btns)

        dlg.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QDialog {{ background-color: {t["bg_primary"]}; }}
            QLabel  {{ color: {t["text_primary"]}; font-size: 13px; }}
            QLineEdit, QComboBox, QSpinBox {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 6px; padding: 0 10px;
            }}
            QLineEdit:focus, QComboBox:focus, QSpinBox:focus {{
                border-color: {t["accent"]};
            }}
        """)

        if dlg.exec() != QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted:
            return

        pattern  = inp_pattern.text().strip()
        mt       = cmb_type.currentData()
        cat_id   = cmb_cat.currentData()
        priority = spn_pri.value()

        if not pattern:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Patrón vacío",
                                "El patrón no puede estar vacío.")
            return

        if cat_id is None:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Sin categoría",
                                "Selecciona una categoría válida.")
            return

        try:
            update_rule(
                self._session,
                rule_id       = rule.id,
                category_id   = cat_id,
                match_pattern = pattern,
                match_type    = mt,
                priority      = priority,
            )
            self.refresh()
            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "Regla actualizada",
                f"Regla <b>'{pattern}'</b> actualizada correctamente."
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error al editar regla", str(exc))

    def _on_delete(self) -> None:
        idx = self._table.currentIndex()
        if not idx.isValid():
            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "Sin selección",
                "Selecciona una regla para eliminar."
            )
            return

        row_idx = idx.row()
        if row_idx >= len(self._rules):
            return

        rule = self._rules[row_idx]
        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self,
            "Confirmar eliminación",
            f"¿Eliminar la regla <b>'{rule.match_pattern}'</b>?",
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes |
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.No
        )
        if reply != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
            return
        try:
            delete_rule(self._session, rule.id)
            self.refresh()
        except ValueError as exc:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", str(exc))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Theme
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def apply_theme(self, theme_key: str) -> None:
        self._theme = theme_key
        self._t = THEMES[theme_key]
        self._apply_theme()

    def _apply_theme(self) -> None:
        t = self._t
        is_dark = self._theme == "dark"
        selection_bg = (
            f"background-color: {t['accent']}; color: #FFFFFF;"
            if is_dark else
            "background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0, stop:0 #DDEBFF, stop:1 #CFE1FF); color: #1E3A8A;"
        )
        selection_hover = (
            f"background-color: {t['accent_hover']}; color: #FFFFFF;"
            if is_dark else
            "background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0, stop:0 #CFE1FF, stop:1 #BFD8FF); color: #1E3A8A;"
        )
        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#dashTopBar {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#dashTitle {{
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary {{
                background-color: {t["accent"]}; color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none; border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnPrimary:hover {{
                background-color: {t["accent_hover"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnDanger {{
                background-color: {t["expense"]}; color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none; border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnDanger:disabled {{
                background-color: {t["border"]};
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary {{
                background-color: transparent;
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
                border: 1px solid {t["border"]};
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnSecondary:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
            }}
            QPushButton#btnAccent {{
                background-color: {t["income"]}; color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none; border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }}
            QPushButton#btnAccent:hover {{
                background-color: #15803D;
            }}
            QTableView {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border: none; color: {t["text_primary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
                alternate-background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
            }}
            QTableView::item {{
                padding: 8px;
                border-bottom: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QTableView::item:hover {{
                background-color: {t["bg_hover"]};
                color: {t["text_primary"]};
            }}
            QTableView::item:selected {{
                {selection_bg}
            }}
            QTableView::item:selected:hover {{
                {selection_hover}
            }}
            QHeaderView::section {{
                background-color: {t["bg_primary"]};
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700;
                padding: 6px 8px; border: none;
                border-bottom: 2px solid {t["border"]};
                text-transform: uppercase;
            }}
            QFrame#rulesBottom {{
                background-color: {t["bg_secondary"]};
                border-top: 1px solid {t["border"]};
            }}
            QLabel#rulesCount {{
                color: {t["text_secondary"]};
                font-size: 12px;
            }}
        """)
```
